Path of Exile 2 0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt: Huntress, 5 New Ascendancies, Endgame & Current Impact

enpublishedverified0.2.0major-updates

0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt was the first large Early Access content and system rebuild: it added the Huntress class, five new ascendancies, 25+ skills, 100+ supports, Spears and Bucklers, 100+ uniques, eight endgame maps, rogue exiles, wisps and socketables. Most of it is still core today.

Quick summary

  • Dawn of the Hunt was the first major content and systems rebuild of Path of Exile 2's Early Access, landing well after the 0.1.x foundation patches. Where 0.1.x had tuned the campaign and Atlas, 0.2.0 reshaped the game: a brand new class, an entire layer of ascendancies, a huge skill and support wave, two new item classes, a large unique injection, and a meaningful endgame expansion. For returning players, this is the update that defined the modern shape of the game.
  • The headline addition was the Huntress, the first wholly new base class of the Early Access, paired with the Spear and Buckler item classes that any class can use. 0.2.0 also delivered five ascendancies across four existing classes: Amazon and Ritualist for the Huntress, Smith of Kitava for the Warrior, Tactician for the Mercenary, and Lich for the Witch. On top of that came 25+ new skills, 100+ new Support gems, 100+ uniques, eight new endgame maps, twelve rogue exiles, Azmerian Wisps, and fifteen socketable items.
  • This article is the total entry for 0.2.0: it documents what shipped, how launch-week hotfixes calmed an unstable opening, the patch family that followed (0.2.0f, 0.2.0h, 0.2.1, then the 0.3 and 0.5 lines), and which pieces of 0.2.0 are still core in client 0.5.4e versus what later patches changed or retired. Facts we could not confirm against current clients are flagged as unknown rather than guessed.
  • First large EA content + system rebuild, not just a balance pass
  • Added the Huntress class, five new ascendancies, and the Spear / Buckler item classes
  • Shipped 25+ skills, 100+ supports, 100+ uniques, 8 maps, 12 rogue exiles, wisps, 15 socketables
  • Most systems remain core today; a few map mechanics are unverified in 0.5.4e

Historical context

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.2.0 era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • Before 0.2.0, Path of Exile 2 Early Access had shipped as a leaner experience. The 0.1.x line added campaign comfort, tower maps, the Arbiter of Ash fight, and quality-of-life like map checkpoints and the /ResetAtlas command, but it did not expand the class or item roster. The game was playable and growing, yet its systems still traced back to launch. 0.2.0 was the moment the design team visibly widened the game's scope.
  • Dawn of the Hunt arrived as the game's first themed league-style content drop. Thematically it leaned into a hunt across the Azmeri wilderness, which is where the Huntress, her spears, and the rogue exiles and wisps fit narratively. For players who started in 0.1.x and stepped away, 0.2.0 is the wall they hit: almost everything downstream assumes the new classes and items exist.
  • Because the update was large and shipped alongside a fresh league, its opening week was volatile. Multiple hotfixes landed in the days after launch to reduce monster and rogue-exile life, fix a Tempest Bell combo requirement, and adjust grenade cooldowns. Those stabilisation changes are part of the 0.2.0 story and are covered in the launch-volatility section below. Later 0.2.0f and 0.2.1 patches then layered on respec, Atlas, and unique-drop improvements.
  • 0.1.x = foundation and comfort; 0.2.0 = first big scope expansion
  • Themed around a hunt across the Azmeri wilderness
  • Launch week needed several hotfixes before the league stabilised

Launch volatility: launch day vs stabilised

Launch-day 0.2.0 (early April 2025)

Non-unique campaign monsters in some level bands felt overtuned, rogue exiles had very high life, Tempest Bell required a 10-hit combo to reach full effect, and Explosive and Voltaic Grenades ran on longer cooldowns. The league felt swingy and some content was slower than intended.

Stabilised 0.2.0 (after launch-week hotfixes)

Hotfixes cut non-unique campaign monster life in affected level bands, further reduced rogue exile life, dropped the Tempest Bell combo requirement from 10 to 4, and lowered Explosive and Voltaic Grenade cooldowns. A Twister multi-projectile bug was also fixed. The league settled into its intended pacing.

Patch family timeline

0.2.02025-04-04Content UpdateDawn of the Hunt: Huntress class, five new ascendancies, 25+ skills, 100+ supports, Spears and Bucklers, 100+ uniques, 8 endgame maps, 12 rogue exiles, Azmerian Wisps, 15 socketables, Twilight Reliquary Key.
0.2.0f2025-04-15Content UpdateAdded Ascendancy respec, Atlas bookmarks, and Charm and Rune drop changes that improved the endgame loop.
0.2.0h2025-05-06Content UpdateFollow-up 0.2.0-line tuning and fixes for the Dawn of the Hunt content set.
0.2.12025-05-27Content UpdateAdded 22 endgame Runes, 7 Talismans, 15 Soul Cores; increased Mythic Unique drops; unique drops no longer consider item class; console item filter support.
0.3.02025-08-01Content UpdateThe Third Edict line that followed the 0.2.x family and continued reshaping content and systems.
0.5.x2026-01-01Content UpdateThe current major line (baseline 0.5.4e) against which this article measures what from 0.2.0 still applies.

流放之路2 0.2.0 猎杀黎明：猎手、5个新升华、终局与当前影响

zh-cnpublishedverified0.2.0major-updates

0.2.0 猎杀黎明是抢先体验首个大型内容与系统重构：新增猎手职业、5个新升华、25+个技能、100+个辅助宝石、长矛与小圆盾、100+件独特装备、8张终局地图、12个流浪放逐者、雾灵与插槽物品。其中大部分至今仍是核心内容。

快速摘要

  • 猎杀黎明是流放之路2抢先体验阶段的第一个大型内容与系统重构，发布时间远晚于 0.1.x 那条奠基性的补丁线。0.1.x 主要调整了战役与异界，而 0.2.0 则是重塑了游戏本身：一个全新职业、整整一层升华、一波庞大的技能与辅助宝石、两个新物品类型，以及可观的终局内容扩充。对回归玩家来说，这个版本定义了游戏现代形态的基础。
  • 最引人注目的新增是猎手（Huntress），这是抢先体验中第一个全新的基础职业，并搭配任何职业都能使用的长矛（Spear）与小圆盾（Buckler）物品类型。0.2.0 还在四个既有职业上带来了五个升华：猎手的亚马逊（Amazon）与仪式师（Ritualist）、 Warrior 的基塔瓦铁匠（Smith of Kitava）、 Mercenary 的战术家（Tactician）、以及 Witch 的巫妖（Lich）。除此之外还有 25+ 个新技能、100+ 个新辅助宝石、100+ 件独特装备、8 张新终局地图、12 个流浪放逐者、雾灵（Azmerian Wisps）与 15 个插槽物品。
  • 本文是 0.2.0 的总览条目：记录实际发布的内容、上线首周热修如何让动荡的开局稳定下来、后续的版本家族（0.2.0f、0.2.0h、0.2.1，再到 0.3 与 0.5 线），以及 0.2.0 的哪些部分在 0.5.4e 客户端中仍是核心、哪些被后续版本改动或淘汰。凡是无法对照当前客户端确认的事实，本文会明确标注为待核实，而不是凭空猜测。
  • 首个大型 EA 内容与系统重构，而非单纯平衡补丁
  • 新增猎手职业、五个新升华，以及长矛 / 小圆盾物品类型
  • 带来 25+ 技能、100+ 辅助宝石、100+ 独特装备、8 张地图、12 个流浪放逐者、雾灵、15 个插槽物品
  • 多数系统至今仍是核心，少数地图机制在 0.5.4e 中待核实

历史背景

流放之路2 抢先体验 —— 0.2.0 时代

本页关于当前状态的描述均以 0.5.4e 客户端为基准。

  • 在 0.2.0 之前，流放之路2 抢先体验以更精简的形态上线。0.1.x 线增加了战役舒适度、塔楼地图、灰烬仲裁者（Arbiter of Ash）战斗，以及地图检查点、/ResetAtlas 命令等生活质量改进，但并未扩充职业或物品阵容。游戏可以游玩且在成长，但其系统仍追溯到首发版本。0.2.0 是设计团队明显拓宽游戏体量的时刻。
  • 猎杀黎明作为游戏首个有主题的联盟式内容掉落登场，主题围绕在阿兹梅里（Azmeri）荒野中的一场狩猎展开，这也正是猎手、她的长矛，以及流浪放逐者与雾灵在叙事上的归宿。对于在 0.1.x 开局后离开、又回归的玩家而言，0.2.0 是他们撞上的那堵墙：其后的几乎所有内容都默认这些新职业与新物品存在。
  • 由于这次更新体量庞大、且伴随全新联盟上线，首周相当动荡。上线后数日内多次热修落地：下调部分等级区间内非独特战役怪物生命、进一步降低流浪放逐者生命、修复风暴钟（Tempest Bell）的连击需求、调整手雷冷却。这些稳定化改动是 0.2.0 故事的一部分，将在下方“上线波动”小节中说明。随后的 0.2.0f 与 0.2.1 又叠加上了重洗、异界与独特掉落改进。
  • 0.1.x = 奠基与舒适度；0.2.0 = 首次大规模扩容
  • 主题围绕阿兹梅里荒野中的一场狩猎
  • 上线首周需要若干热修，联盟才稳定下来

上线波动：上线当天 vs 稳定后

0.2.0 上线当天（2025年4月初）

部分等级区间的非独特战役怪物强度偏高，流浪放逐者生命极高，风暴钟需要 10 次连击才能打出满额效果，爆裂手雷与电涌手雷的冷却较长。联盟给人的感觉起伏很大，部分内容推进比预期更慢。

稳定后的 0.2.0（上线首周热修后）

热修下调了受影响等级区间的非独特战役怪物生命，进一步降低流浪放逐者生命，把风暴钟连击需求从 10 降到 4，并缩短了爆裂手雷与电涌手雷的冷却。旋风（Twister）多重投射物 bug 也得到修复。联盟节奏回到了预期水平。

版本家族时间线

0.2.02025-04-04内容更新猎杀黎明：猎手职业、五个新升华、25+ 技能、100+ 辅助宝石、长矛与小圆盾、100+ 独特装备、8 张终局地图、12 个流浪放逐者、雾灵、15 个插槽物品、暮光圣物钥匙（Twilight Reliquary Key）。
0.2.0f2025-04-15内容更新新增升华重洗、异界书签，以及护符与符文掉落改动，改善了终局循环。
0.2.0h2025-05-06内容更新针对猎杀黎明内容集的 0.2.0 线后续调整与修复。
0.2.12025-05-27内容更新新增 22 个终局符文、7 个护身符（Talisman）、15 个魂核（Soul Cores）；提升神话独特掉落；独特掉落不再考虑物品类型；主机支持物品过滤。
0.3.02025-08-01内容更新第三敕令线，承接 0.2.x 家族，持续推进内容与系统重塑。
0.5.x2026-01-01内容更新当前主线（基准 0.5.4e），本文以它衡量 0.2.0 内容的当前适用性。

PoE2 0.2.0 Huntress & New Ascendancies: Amazon, Ritualist, Lich, Tactician, Smith of Kitava — Origins & Current State

enpublishedverified0.2.0major-updates

0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt introduced the Huntress class, five new ascendancies (Amazon, Ritualist, Smith of Kitava, Tactician, Lich), and the Spear and Buckler item classes. This system-origin article explains where they came from and what is still true in 0.5.4e.

Quick summary

  • This is a system-origin article, not a build guide. It explains where the Huntress class, the five 0.2.0 ascendancies, and the Spear and Buckler item classes came from, and which of those facts still hold in client 0.5.4e. If you want a playable setup, pair this page with the linked builds such as Lightning Spear Amazon or Glacial Lance Ritualist.
  • 0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt shipped the Huntress as the first new base class of Early Access, and at the same time delivered five ascendancies across four existing classes: Amazon and Ritualist for the Huntress, Smith of Kitava for the Warrior, Tactician for the Mercenary, and Lich for the Witch. It also introduced Spears and Bucklers as entirely new item classes that any class can equip.
  • Two ideas trip up returning players. First, a Spear build does not have to be a Huntress; Spears are a universal weapon class. Second, Bucklers are a distinct off-hand item class, not the same as Shields, and like Spears they are usable by any class. Both points matter when you set up weapon-set passives, which is why this article links the weapon-set-passive-points guide.
  • Huntress + five new ascendancies all originated in 0.2.0
  • Spears and Bucklers are universal item classes, not Huntress-locked
  • Old build videos may show node numbers that no longer match the current tree
  • Pair with linked builds and the weapon-set passive guide for a usable setup

System origin: Huntress & new ascendancies

0.2.0

official-0-2-0

  • The Huntress and her five co-shipped ascendancies were introduced together in the 0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt content update. The class was designed around martial spear combat and a buckler off-hand, which is why the Spear and Buckler item classes landed in the same patch. The five ascendancies were not scattered across later patches; they arrived as a single batch in 0.2.0, three for entirely new class identity and two extending existing classes.
  • Amazon and Ritualist are the two Huntress-specific ascendancies, giving the class both a physical/crit spear identity and a bleed/ritual identity. The other three extend classes that already existed: Smith of Kitava for the Warrior, Tactician for the Mercenary, and Lich for the Witch. That split matters because it means 0.2.0 changed not just one class but the ascendancy identity of four base classes at once.
  • Because the whole layer shipped in one patch, any guide or video made before 0.2.0 cannot reference these ascendancies at all. After 0.2.0 they became permanent fixtures of the class roster, and later patches (0.3, 0.5) have tuned them but have not removed them. Their current node layouts, however, should be checked against the live tree rather than old footage.
  • Huntress, Amazon, Ritualist, Smith of Kitava, Tactician, Lich all entered in 0.2.0
  • Two ascendancies are Huntress-specific; three extend existing classes
  • All five remain in the roster in 0.5.4e, but node numbers may have shifted

Passive tree impact

  • The 0.2.0 class and item expansion added new passive clusters to support the Huntress, the new ascendancies, and Spear/Buckler play. Huntress-specific clusters cover spear attack speed, bleed, and crit, while the other new ascendancies gained their own dedicated nodes on the existing class trees.
  • Weapon-set passive points are the part most likely to confuse returning players. Because Spears and Bucklers are universal item classes, a character can carry a Spear weapon set and earn passive nodes that only activate while that set is equipped. The exact node numbers and cluster positions have shifted across 0.3 and 0.5 balance passes, so older passive-tree screenshots from 0.2.0 are not reliable.
  • For a usable layout, start from a current build page (such as Lightning Spear Amazon or Glacial Lance Ritualist) and the weapon-set-passive-points-explained guide rather than a 0.2.0-era planner. The ascendancy identities are unchanged even when individual node math has moved.
  • New clusters for Huntress, the five ascendancies, and Spear/Buckler play
  • Weapon-set passive points gate on equipped Spear/Buckler sets
  • Node numbers shifted in 0.3/0.5; trust current planners

流放之路2 0.2.0 猎手与新升华：亚马逊、仪式师、巫妖、战术家、基塔瓦铁匠的来源与现状

zh-cnpublishedverified0.2.0major-updates

0.2.0 猎杀黎明引入了猎手职业、五个新升华（亚马逊、仪式师、基塔瓦铁匠、战术家、巫妖）以及长矛与小圆盾物品类型。本系统来源文章说明它们从何而来，以及哪些事实在 0.5.4e 中仍然成立。

快速摘要

  • 这是一篇系统来源文章，而非构筑指南。它解释猎手职业、五个 0.2.0 升华，以及长矛与小圆盾物品类型的来源，以及这些事实哪些在 0.5.4e 客户端中仍然成立。如果你想要可玩的配装，请把本页与闪电长矛亚马逊、冰枪仪式师等关联构筑结合使用。
  • 0.2.0 猎杀黎明将猎手作为抢先体验首个新基础职业推出，同时还在四个既有职业上带来了五个升华：猎手的亚马逊与仪式师、Warrior 的基塔瓦铁匠、Mercenary 的战术家，以及 Witch 的巫妖。它还把长矛与小圆盾作为全新物品类型引入，任何职业都能装备。
  • 有两点常让回归玩家踩坑。第一，长矛构筑不一定要选猎手，长矛是通用武器类型。第二，小圆盾是一个独立的副手物品类型，与盾牌不同，和长矛一样任何职业都能用。这两点当你配置武器配置被动时都很关键，这也是本文关联武器配置被动指南的原因。
  • 猎手 + 五个新升华都源自 0.2.0
  • 长矛与小圆盾是通用物品类型，并非猎手专属
  • 旧构筑视频的节点数值可能已与当前天赋树不符
  • 配合关联构筑与武器配置被动指南获得可用配装

系统来源：猎手与新升华

0.2.0

official-0-2-0

  • 猎手与同期五个新升华是在 0.2.0 猎杀黎明内容更新中一起引入的。该职业围绕武术长矛战斗与小圆盾副手设计，这也解释了长矛与小圆盾物品类型为何在同一补丁落地。这五个升华并非分散在后续补丁，而是作为一批在 0.2.0 中整体到来：两个构成全新职业身份，三个则扩展既有职业。
  • 亚马逊与仪式师是猎手专属的两个升华，分别赋予职业物理/暴击长矛身份与流血/仪式身份。其余三个扩展了已经存在的职业：Warrior 的基塔瓦铁匠、Mercenary 的战术家、Witch 的巫妖。这个划分很重要，因为它意味着 0.2.0 不仅改了一个职业，而是一次性改变了四个基础职业的升华身份。
  • 由于整层都在一个补丁中落地，任何 0.2.0 之前制作的指南或视频完全无法引用这些升华。在 0.2.0 之后它们成为职业阵容的永久成员，后续版本（0.3、0.5）对其进行了调整但未移除。不过它们当前的节点布局应比照实时天赋树，而非旧录像。
  • 猎手、亚马逊、仪式师、基塔瓦铁匠、战术家、巫妖都在 0.2.0 进入游戏
  • 两个升华为猎手专属；三个扩展既有职业
  • 五个升华在 0.5.4e 中仍在阵容，但节点数值可能已变动

天赋树影响

  • 0.2.0 的职业与物品扩充新增了支持猎手、新升华，以及长矛/小圆盾玩法的天赋簇。猎手专属簇覆盖长矛攻击速度、流血与暴击，其余新升华在既有职业天赋树上获得了自己的专属节点。
  • 武器配置被动点是最容易让回归玩家困惑的部分。由于长矛与小圆盾是通用物品类型，角色可以持有一套长矛武器配置，并获得仅在该配置装备时生效的被动节点。精确的节点数值与簇位置在 0.3 与 0.5 平衡中已变动，因此 0.2.0 时代的旧天赋树截图并不可靠。
  • 要获得可用布局，请从当前构筑页（如闪电长矛亚马逊或冰枪仪式师）与 weapon-set-passive-points-explained 指南入手，而非 0.2.0 时代的规划器。即便个别节点数值已移动，升华身份并未改变。
  • 为猎手、五个升华及长矛/小圆盾玩法新增了天赋簇
  • 武器配置被动点以装备的长矛/小圆盾配置为生效前提
  • 节点数值在 0.3/0.5 中变动；请信任当前规划器

PoE2 0.2.0 Endgame & Atlas: Azmerian Wisps, Rogue Exiles, Strongboxes & Old Map Rules

enpublishedverified0.2.0major-updates

A historical reference for the 0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt endgame: Azmerian Wisps, 12 Rogue Exiles, Unique Strongboxes, 8 Endgame Maps, and the Twilight Reliquary Key, plus which of those rules survive into 0.5.4e.

Quick summary

  • Patch 0.2.0, titled Dawn of the Hunt, arrived as the second major content update for Path of Exile 2 and reshaped the endgame Atlas in ways that early-access players still reference today. This article collects the historical origins of the 0.2.0 endgame systems - Azmerian Wisps, Rogue Exiles, Unique Strongboxes, the first batch of Endgame Maps, and the Twilight Reliquary Key - and explains which of those rules survive into the current 0.5.4e baseline.
  • Many of these systems have since been superseded. The Tablet workflow described by old Atlas guides was rewritten in 0.3.1, and later endgame overhauls in 0.4 and 0.5 changed how maps, towers, and rewards scale. Treat this page as a historical reference for what 0.2.0 actually shipped, not as a current how-to.
  • If you are returning after a long break, the most important takeaway is that Wisps and Rogue Exiles were introduced here and remained part of the endgame, while the specific Atlas layout, Strongbox behaviour, and Reliquary access have drifted from these original rules.
  • Azmerian Wisps, 12 Rogue Exiles, and Unique Strongboxes all debuted in 0.2.0.
  • 8 Endgame Maps were added in this patch.
  • Twilight Reliquary Key was the original gate to the Reliquary Vault.
  • Tablet and map rules from this era were later rewritten (see superseded changes).

Historical context: the early Atlas

Early Access, pre-0.3.1 Atlas

Compared against the current 0.5.4e endgame baseline.

  • When Dawn of the Hunt launched, the endgame Atlas was still in its first mature form. The 0.1.x line had established Waystones, Towers, and the first Pinnacle bosses; 0.2.0 then layered a set of roaming and map-embedded reward systems on top of that foundation. Players who had learned the 0.1 Atlas now had to account for monsters that fled, exiles that used Unique gear, and chests that could roll Unique.
  • This era matters because a large share of community guides, boss-kill videos, and farming route write-ups were created during 0.2.0. Many of those resources describe Atlas interactions that no longer behave the same way. Understanding the original design helps you judge whether an old guide is still usable or needs a revision pass.
  • The historical baseline here is the 0.5.4e client. Anything described as current in this article reflects that version, not 0.2.0. Where a 0.2.0 rule still applies, we flag it; where it was replaced, we name the patch that replaced it.
  • 0.1.x built Waystones, Towers, and early Pinnacles.
  • 0.2.0 added roaming and map-embedded reward systems.
  • Most early Atlas guide videos date from this window.

How the 0.2.0 Atlas flowed

  • The 0.2.0 Atlas flow began, as before, by running Waystone maps. The new twist was that the maps themselves could contain special content that transformed a routine run into a high-value encounter. Wisps drifted across the map, Rogue Exiles patrolled with Unique gear, and Strongboxes could appear as Unique variants worth farming.
  • Crucially, these were not isolated boss fights. They were layered onto existing map progression, so a single Waystone could escalate from a normal clear to a reward-dense spree if you chased the right signals. This design rewarded map knowledge and spatial awareness more than raw character power.
  • The 8 Endgame Maps added in 0.2.0 expanded the pool of high-tier content and gave players more destinations on the Atlas. Combined with Tablets, which were then placed in Towers, they defined how a 0.2.0 farming route was built. That Tower-based Tablet workflow is covered in the superseded-changes section because it no longer exists.
  • Waystone maps remained the entry point.
  • Wisps, Rogue Exiles, and Unique Strongboxes layered onto map runs.
  • 8 new Endgame Maps expanded the high-tier pool.

System origin: Azmerian Wisps

0.2.0 (Dawn of the Hunt)

official-0-2-0-notes

  • Azmerian Wisps were introduced in 0.2.0 as roaming entities that flee from the player and seek out nearby monsters. When a Wisp reaches a monster, it possesses or buffs that monster, visibly upgrading it and marking it as a higher-value target.
  • The core loop is pursuit and escalation. You chase the Wisp, it empowers the monsters it touches, and those empowered monsters drop better rewards when slain. This created a distinctive hunt-the-glowing-thing gameplay beat that distinguished 0.2.0 map clearing from the more static 0.1 experience.
  • Because Wisps move and choose their hosts, they introduce randomness into reward density. A map could swing from dull to lucrative depending on which monsters the Wisps found. This unpredictability was a deliberate design goal of the system's origin.
  • Wisps flee from the player and seek monsters.
  • They buff possessed monsters and raise their reward tier.
  • Slaying buffed monsters yields increased rewards.
  • Reward density becomes map-dependent and emergent.

System origin: Rogue Exiles

0.2.0 (Dawn of the Hunt)

official-0-2-0-notes

  • Rogue Exiles arrived in 0.2.0 as a set of twelve recurring NPC hunters, each wielding a full Unique-item loadout. Unlike random rare monsters, Rogue Exiles were designed characters with defined gear, so fighting one felt like dueling a player-built character.
  • Their reward design mirrored that identity: because they wore Uniques, killing them could drop those Uniques and other valuable loot. They became a sought-after encounter for players chasing specific Unique items or simply high-value farm runs.
  • The twelve-Exile structure gave the system room for variety across the Atlas. Different Exiles favoured different skills and gear, so players learned to recognise them and adjust tactics. This is one of the 0.2.0 systems that survived into the current baseline, though individual behaviours have been tuned in later patches.
  • 12 Rogue Exiles, each with a Unique-item loadout.
  • They drop rewards tied to their Unique gear.
  • Recognisable, character-like fights across the Atlas.
  • Survives into 0.5.4e with later tuning.

流放之路2 0.2.0 终局与Atlas：阿兹梅里幽光、流放者、强箱与旧地图规则

zh-cnpublishedverified0.2.0major-updates

一份关于 0.2.0「猎杀黎明」终局的历史参考：阿兹梅里幽光、12 名流放者、独特强箱、8 张终局地图以及暮光圣物密钥，并说明这些规则中有哪些延续到了 0.5.4e。

快速摘要

  • 0.2.0 版本「猎杀黎明」（Dawn of the Hunt）是流放之路2 的第二波大型内容更新，它以早期玩家至今仍在引用的方式重塑了终局 Atlas。本文整理了 0.2.0 终局系统的历史起源——阿兹梅里幽光、流放者、独特强箱、首批终局地图，以及暮光圣物密钥——并说明其中哪些规则延续到了当前的 0.5.4e 基线。
  • 这些系统中很多后来都已被取代。旧 Atlas 指南里描述的平板（Tablet）流程在 0.3.1 被重写，而 0.4 和 0.5 的终局大改又改变了地图、高塔与奖励的运作方式。请把本页当作对 0.2.0 实际内容的史料参考，而不是当下的操作教程。
  • 如果你在长时间离开后回归，最重要的结论是：幽光与流放者都是在这个版本引入并保留进了终局，而具体的 Atlas 布局、强箱行为以及圣物库的入口，都已经偏离了最初的规则。
  • 阿兹梅里幽光、12 名流放者、独特强箱都在 0.2.0 首次登场。
  • 本版本新增了 8 张终局地图。
  • 暮光圣物密钥原本是开启圣物库（Reliquary Vault）的钥匙。
  • 这一时期的平板与地图规则后来被改写（见被取代的变更）。

历史背景：早期的 Atlas

抢先体验期，0.3.1 之前的 Atlas

对照当前的 0.5.4e 终局基线。

  • 「猎杀黎明」上线时，终局 Atlas 仍处于它第一个成熟形态。0.1.x 系列已经建立了 Wayne stone（界石）、高塔以及最早的巅峰 Boss；0.2.0 在此基础上叠加了一整套漫游式与地图内嵌式的奖励系统。曾熟悉 0.1 Atlas 的玩家，现在必须面对会逃跑的怪物、使用独特装备的流放者，以及可能开出 Unique 的宝箱。
  • 这一时期之所以重要，是因为大量社区攻略、击杀视频与刷图路线写法都诞生于 0.2.0。其中许多资料描述的 Atlas 互动方式已不再相同。理解最初的设计，有助于你判断一份旧攻略是否仍然可用、是否需要修订。
  • 本文的对照基线是 0.5.4e 客户端。任何被描述为「当前」的内容都对应这一版本，而非 0.2.0。当某条 0.2.0 规则仍然有效时我们会标注；当它被取代时，我们会点名取代它的版本。
  • 0.1.x 建立了界石、高塔与早期巅峰 Boss。
  • 0.2.0 加入了漫游式与地图内嵌式的奖励系统。
  • 多数早期 Atlas 攻略视频都出自这一窗口期。

0.2.0 Atlas 的运转方式

  • 0.2.0 的 Atlas 流程依旧以跑界石地图作为入口。新的变化在于，地图本身可以包含特殊内容，把一次普通的清图变成高收益的遭遇战。幽光在地图中飘移，流放者带着独特装备巡逻，强箱也可能以 Unique 变体出现，值得专门farm。
  • 关键在于，这些都不是孤立的 Boss 战，而是叠加在既有地图进程之上的。所以一张界石地图只要追对信号，就能从普通清图升级成奖励密集的连刷。这种设计更奖励对地图的了解与空间意识，而非单纯的角色强度。
  • 0.2.0 新增的 8 张终局地图扩充了高层内容池，给了玩家更多 Atlas 上的目的地。配合当时放进高塔的平板，它们定义了 0.2.0 刷图路线的搭建方式。那种「平板放进高塔」的流程会在「被取代的变更」一节说明，因为它已经不复存在。
  • 界石地图仍是入口。
  • 幽光、流放者、独特强箱叠加在地图进程中。
  • 新增的 8 张终局地图扩充了高层内容池。

系统起源：阿兹梅里幽光

0.2.0（猎杀黎明）

official-0-2-0-notes

  • 阿兹梅里幽光在 0.2.0 作为漫游实体登场：它们会逃离玩家，去寻找附近的怪物。当幽光接触到一只怪物时，会附身或强化它，使其明显升级，并标记为更高价值的击杀目标。
  • 核心循环是追逐与升级。你追着幽光跑，它强化所碰到的怪物，而被强化的怪物在被击杀时会掉落更好的奖励。这营造出一种「追着发光的东西跑」的独特节奏，让 0.2.0 的清图体验区别于更静态的 0.1。
  • 由于幽光会移动并自行选择寄宿对象，它们把随机性引入了奖励密度。一张地图可能随着幽光找到的怪物，从平淡无奇瞬间变成收益丰厚。这种不可预测性正是该系统最初设计的刻意目标。
  • 幽光逃离玩家并寻找怪物。
  • 它们强化被附身的怪物并提升其奖励层级。
  • 击杀被强化的怪物会掉落更多奖励。
  • 奖励密度变得依赖地图且具涌现性。

系统起源：流放者

0.2.0（猎杀黎明）

official-0-2-0-notes

  • 流放者在 0.2.0 作为一组共十二名反复出现的 NPC 猎手登场，每人都配齐了一整套独特（Unique）装备。与随机的稀有怪物不同，流放者是设计好的角色，拥有明确的配装，所以与之战斗就像在和一名玩家打造的角色决斗。
  • 它们的奖励设计也呼应了这种身份：因为它们身穿 Unique，击杀它们就可能掉落这些 Unique 以及其他有价值的战利品。对于追逐特定 Unique 或单纯想要高收益刷图的玩家而言，它们成了抢手的遭遇战。
  • 十二名流放者的结构让系统在整个 Atlas 上拥有变化空间。不同的流放者偏好不同的技能与配装，玩家因此学会辨认他们并调整战术。这是少数从 0.2.0 保留进当前基线、并在后续版本中被微调的系统之一。
  • 12 名流放者，每人配齐一套独特装备。
  • 它们的掉落与身上的独特装备挂钩。
  • 在 Atlas 上是可辨认、接近玩家对战风格的战斗。
  • 保留进 0.5.4e，后续有平衡调整。

强箱与新增终局地图

今昔对比

[

 {

PoE2 0.2.0f: How to Respec Ascendancy, Use Atlas Bookmarks, and Why Charm & Rune Drops Changed

enpublishedverified0.2.0fmajor-updates

A historical how-to for 0.2.0f: respeccing your Ascendancy via a qualifying trial and the Ascension Device, using Atlas Bookmarks, and why Charm slots and Lesser/Medium Rune drops changed at high tier.

Quick answer: how to respec

  • Patch 0.2.0f added the ability to respec your Ascendancy class, a change many players had requested since early access began. The short answer: you do not respec by talking to an NPC. You must complete an Ascendancy trial that meets the points requirement, then use the Ascension Device to refund your points and choose a new Ascendancy.
  • This article explains the full 0.2.0f flow, the Atlas Bookmarks that also arrived in this patch, and the Charm and Rune drop changes that surprised players. Because 0.2.0f is a historical patch, we note which behaviours still apply in 0.5.4e and which were later adjusted.
  • If your only goal is the respec, jump to the migration guide below. If your old belt's Charm slots did not update, or you stopped seeing Lesser Runes at high level, the change-explorer and FAQ sections cover those cases.
  • Ascendancy Respec, Atlas Bookmarks, and Charm or Rune drops all changed in 0.2.0f.
  • Respec requires completing a qualifying Ascendancy trial.
  • Old belts do not automatically gain the new Charm slot behaviour.

Historical context: version facts

Early Access, 0.2.0f hotfix window

Compared against the current 0.5.4e baseline.

  • 0.2.0f was a focused follow-up to the large Dawn of the Hunt content update. While 0.2.0 introduced whole systems, 0.2.0f concentrated on quality-of-life and drop-rate corrections that players had flagged during the first weeks of the league.
  • The headline features were Ascendancy Respecialisation and Atlas Bookmarks, but the patch also adjusted how Charms and Runes drop. These drop changes are the kind of silent adjustment that old guides miss, so we document them here as historical fact.
  • As with other historical patches, the current baseline is 0.5.4e. Some 0.2.0f rules still hold; others were refined in later patches. The current-applicability section lists which is which.
  • 0.2.0f followed the 0.2.0 Dawn of the Hunt update.
  • Focused on QoL: Ascendancy respec and Atlas Bookmarks.
  • Also adjusted Charm and Rune drop behaviour.

Atlas Bookmarks

  • Atlas Bookmarks were added in 0.2.0f as a way to save and jump between points of interest on your Atlas. Each bookmark stores a location you can return to through the Atlas navigation, reducing the friction of repeated farming routes.
  • Bookmarks appear as icons you place on the Atlas with a label, so you can name a favourite map, a Tower, or a farming cluster and revisit it without re-finding the spot manually. The exact number of bookmarks available and the icon set are details we mark as unverified, because later patches adjusted the Atlas UI.
  • In practice, bookmarks turned the Atlas from a map you re-explored each session into a set of saved waypoints. This was a quiet but meaningful quality-of-life win for players running the same rotations daily.
  • Bookmarks save Atlas locations for quick return.
  • Each bookmark has an icon and a label.
  • Bookmark count and exact icon set are 待核实 for 0.2.0f.

Charm slots: before and after

Before 0.2.0f (existing belts)

Belts crafted before the Charm-slot change already had their slot configuration. They were built under the old Charm rules.

After 0.2.0f (new belts)

New belts gain the updated Charm slot behaviour. Belts that existed before the change do not automatically receive the new slots; you must obtain or craft a new belt to get them.

流放之路2 0.2.0f：如何重选升华、使用Atlas书签，以及护符与符文掉率为何改变

zh-cnpublishedverified0.2.0fmajor-updates

一份关于 0.2.0f 的历史操作指南：通过合格试炼与升华装置重选升华，使用 Atlas 书签，以及护符槽与低阶符文掉率在高阶为何改变。

快速回答：如何重选

  • 0.2.0f 添加了重选升华（Ascendancy）职业的能力，这是抢先体验开启后许多玩家一直呼吁的改动。简短回答是：你不能靠找 NPC 对话来重选。你必须完成一场满足点数要求的升华试炼，然后使用升华装置（Ascension Device）退还点数并选择新的升华。
  • 本文完整说明 0.2.0f 的流程、同期加入的 Atlas 书签，以及让玩家措手不及的护符与符文掉落改动。由于 0.2.0f 是历史版本，我们会标注哪些行为在 0.5.4e 中仍然有效、哪些后来被调整。
  • 如果你的目标仅仅是重选升华，请直接跳到下方迁移指南。如果你的旧腰带护符槽没有更新，或你到了高层就不再见到低阶符文，变更浏览器与常见问题两节会覆盖这些情况。
  • 升华重选、Atlas 书签、护符或符文掉落都在 0.2.0f 发生改变。
  • 重选需要完成一场合格的升华试炼。
  • 旧腰带不会自动获得新的护符槽行为。

历史背景：版本事实

抢先体验期，0.2.0f 热修窗口

对照当前的 0.5.4e 基线。

  • 0.2.0f 是大型「猎杀黎明」内容更新之后一次聚焦的后续补丁。0.2.0 引入的是整套系统，而 0.2.0f 集中在玩家在联赛头几周反馈的生活质量与掉率修正上。
  • 最受瞩目的功能是升华重选与 Atlas 书签，但该补丁也调整了护符与符文的掉落方式。这类掉落改动正是旧攻略容易遗漏的「静默调整」，因此我们在此作为历史事实记录。
  • 与其他历史补丁一样，当前基线是 0.5.4e。部分 0.2.0f 规则仍然成立；另一些在后续版本中被细化。当前适用性一节会列出哪些是哪些。
  • 0.2.0f 紧接 0.2.0 猎杀黎明更新。
  • 聚焦生活质量：升华重选与 Atlas 书签。
  • 同时调整了护符与符文的掉落行为。

Atlas 书签

  • Atlas 书签在 0.2.0f 加入，用来保存并快速跳转 Atlas 上你感兴趣的点位。每个书签记录一个位置，你可以通过 Atlas 导航返回，减少重复刷图路线的摩擦。
  • 书签以图标形式出现在你在 Atlas 上放置的位置，并带有标签，因此你可以为一个心仪的地图、一座高塔或一个刷图集群命名，之后无需手动重新寻找该处。可用的书签具体数量与图标集合是我们标记为待核实的细节，因为后续版本调整过 Atlas 界面。
  • 在实践中，书签把 Atlas 从一张每局都要重新探索的地图，变成了一组已保存的路点。对每天跑相同循环的玩家来说，这是一次安静却意义重大的生活质量提升。
  • 书签保存 Atlas 位置以便快速返回。
  • 每个书签都有图标与标签。
  • 书签数量与具体图标集合在 0.2.0f 中待核实。

护符槽：改动前后

0.2.0f 之前（既有腰带）

在护符槽改动之前打造的腰带，已经拥有自己的槽位配置，它们是在旧护符规则下构建的。

0.2.0f 之后（新腰带）

新腰带获得更新后的护符槽行为。改动前已存在的腰带不会自动获得新槽位；你必须获取或打造一条新腰带才能得到它们。

符文掉落进度

PoE2 0.2.0 Launch Hotfix Timeline: Monster Life, Tempest Bell, Grenades & Week-1 Build Shifts

enpublishedverified0.2.0hotfixes

Why so many 0.2.0 day-one guides went stale within a week: the launch-week hotfix chain cut campaign monster life, dropped Tempest Bell from 10 to 4 combo, lowered Grenade cooldowns, and fixed a Twister bug.

Why day-one 0.2.0 guides aged badly

  • When Path of Exile 2 launched the Dawn of the Hunt content update (0.2.0) on April 4, 2025, the community moved fast. Leveling routes, campaign monster-life references, and a first wave of Spear and Grenade builds were written against the exact state of the game on day one.
  • Within days, a launch-week hotfix chain rewrote parts of that math. Campaign monster life was trimmed in several level bands, Rogue Exiles were cut further, the Tempest Bell combo requirement fell from 10 to 4, Grenade cooldowns were lowered, and a Twister multi-projectile bug was fixed. Guides dated before those fixes were quietly wrong.
  • This article merges that launch-week chain into a single stabilization timeline so you can see, at a glance, which claims in old guides are pre-fix and which survived. We treat 0.2.0f as the first broadly stable baseline and cross-link to it; later 0.2.0h continued the cleanup but sits outside this article's focal scope.
  • If a video you are watching shows a 10-combo Bell, a sluggish Grenade cadence, or monster clumps that feel tankier than your live client, you are almost certainly watching pre-fix footage. The sections below map each change to the builds and guides it broke or improved.
  • Launch-week hotfixes changed combat math within days of 0.2.0 going live.
  • Tempest Bell dropped from 10 combo to 4 combo after launch.
  • Explosive and Voltaic Grenade cooldowns were lowered during launch week.
  • Twister's multi-projectile bug was fixed, changing clear-speed benchmarks.
  • 0.2.0f is used here as the first stable baseline for 0.2.0 content.

Stabilization timeline (Day 1 to 0.2.0h)

0.2.0 (Day 1)2025-04-04Content UpdateDawn of the Hunt launched: Huntress, new Ascendancies, 25+ skills, 100+ Supports, Spears and Bucklers, 100+ Uniques, 8 Endgame Maps, 12 Rogue Exiles, and Azmerian Wisps.
0.2.0 Hotfix 6 (launch week)2025-04 (launch week; exact day unverified)HotfixFirst rapid-response fixes: campaign monster-life reductions in some level bands, Rogue Exile life further reduced, Tempest Bell combo 10 to 4, Grenade cooldown reductions, and the Twister multi-projectile fix.
0.2.0b2025-04 (launch month; exact day unverified)HotfixContinued launch-week stabilization addressing balance and crash reports that followed the initial player surge.
0.2.0f2025-04 (late launch month; exact day unverified)Content HotfixFocal stable baseline for this article: added Ascendancy Respecialization, Atlas Bookmarks, and Charm/Rune drop changes on top of the earlier launch-week fixes.
0.2.0hunknownHotfixLater stabilization hotfix in the 0.2.0 family. Listed for completeness; detailed auditing is out of scope for this 0.2.0f-focused article.

流放之路2 0.2.0 上线热修时间线：怪物生命、钟声、榴弹与首周Build变化

zh-cnpublishedverified0.2.0hotfixes

为什么那么多 0.2.0 首日攻略在一周内就过时了：上线周热修链削减了战役怪物生命、把钟声连击需求从 10 降到 4、降低了榴弹冷却，并修复了龙卷风的多重投射物 bug。

为什么 0.2.0 首日攻略这么快就过时

  • 2025 年 4 月 4 日流放之路2 推出「猎捕黎明」资料片（0.2.0）时，社区的反应非常迅速。升级路线、战役怪物生命参考，以及第一批长矛与榴弹 Build，都是对照游戏首日当天的确切状态写出来的。
  • 然而在几天之内，一条上线周热修链就重写了其中的部分数值。战役怪物生命在多个等级区间被削减，流放者（Rogue Exile）被进一步削弱，钟声的连击需求从 10 降到 4，榴弹冷却被降低，龙卷风的多重投射物 bug 也被修复。那些在修复之前写好的攻略，悄然出现了错误。
  • 本文把这条上线周热修链合并成一份稳定化时间线，让你一眼看清旧攻略里哪些说法是修复前的、哪些又保留了下来。我们把 0.2.0f 视为首个相对稳定的基线并进行交叉引用；更晚的 0.2.0h 虽然继续做了清理，但不在本文的重点范围内。
  • 如果你正在看的视频里出现需要 10 连击的钟声、迟缓的榴弹节奏，或者比你当前客户端里更肉的怪物群，那几乎可以肯定你看到的是修复前的画面。下面的小节会把每一次改动映射到受它影响或改善的 Build 与攻略上。
  • 上线周热修在 0.2.0 上线几天内就改变了战斗数值。
  • 钟声的连击需求在上线后从 10 降到 4。
  • 爆发榴弹与电压榴弹的冷却在上线周被降低。
  • 龙卷风的多重投射物 bug 被修复，改变了清屏速度基准。
  • 本文以 0.2.0f 作为 0.2.0 内容的首个稳定基线。

稳定化时间线（首日 到 0.2.0h）

0.2.0（首日）2025-04-04Content Update猎捕黎明上线：女猎手、新升华、25 个以上新技能、100 多个辅助宝石、长矛与轻盾、100 多个独特装备、8 张终局地图、12 个流放者，以及阿兹梅里幽光。
0.2.0 Hotfix 6（上线周）2025-04（上线周；具体日期待核实）Hotfix首批快速响应修复：部分等级区间的战役怪物生命削减、流放者生命进一步降低、钟声连击 10 降为 4、榴弹冷却降低，以及龙卷风多重投射物修复。
0.2.0b2025-04（上线当月；具体日期待核实）Hotfix继续上线周的稳定化工作，处理开服玩家涌入后报告的平衡与崩溃问题。
0.2.0f2025-04（上线当月下旬；具体日期待核实）Content Hotfix本文的重点稳定基线：在更早的上线周修复之上，新增了升华洗点、 atlas 书签，以及护符与符文掉落改动。
0.2.0hunknownHotfix0.2.0 家族中更晚的稳定化热修。列出仅供参考；详细核查不在本文以 0.2.0f 为重点的范围内。

PoE2 0.2.1: 22 Runes, 7 Talismans, 15 Soul Cores, and How Unique Drops Changed

enpublishedverified0.2.1balance

0.2.1 overhauled the loot and socketable systems: 22 Endgame Runes, 7 Talismans and 15 Soul Cores arrived, while Unique drops stopped considering Item Class, Mythic Uniques rose, and the party Unique bonus was cut.

Quick summary

  • Patch 0.2.1 was the loot and socketable overhaul of the Dawn of the Hunt era. It introduced three whole new families of socketable items: 22 Endgame Runes, 7 Talismans, and 15 Soul Cores, each meant to be slotted into gear for new modifiers and build options.
  • Alongside the socketables, 0.2.1 rewrote how Unique items drop. The Unique generation pipeline stopped considering Item Class, which made Rings and Amulets show up more often. A Mythic Unique increase pushed more top-tier drops into circulation, high-tier areas began reducing the share of Common Uniques, and the party-play Unique bonus was reduced.
  • Console players got a quiet but important quality-of-life win: Item Filter support arrived, which matters directly because the new socketables will not appear in an old filter until you add entries for them. This article explains each change in player terms and flags what is still true versus what later patches may have adjusted.
  • No drop-rate percentages are stated here. The official notes describe these as rule changes, not published numbers, so every figure below is described by direction and mechanism only.
  • 0.2.1 added 22 Endgame Runes, 7 Talismans, and 15 Soul Cores.
  • Unique drops no longer consider Item Class, so Rings and Amulets are more common.
  • Mythic Unique drop rate increased; high-tier areas reduce Common Unique share.
  • Party Unique bonus was reduced.
  • Console Item Filter support means new socketables need filter entries.

流放之路2 0.2.1：22符文、7护符、15魂核，以及独特掉落如何改变

zh-cnpublishedverified0.2.1balance

0.2.1 重做了掉落与镶嵌物系统：新增 22 个终局符文、7 个护符、15 个魂核，同时独特掉落不再考虑物品类别、神话独特增加、队伍独特加成被削减。

快速摘要

  • 0.2.1 是猎捕黎明时代的掉落与镶嵌物大改。它引入了三个全新的镶嵌物家族：22 个终局符文、7 个护符，以及 15 个魂核，每一种都可以镶嵌进装备，提供新的词缀与 Build 选择。
  • 在新增镶嵌物的同时，0.2.1 重写了独特物品的掉落方式。独特生成管线不再考虑物品类别，使戒指与护符出现得更频繁；神话独特的掉落率被提高；高层区域开始降低普通独特的占比；队伍的独特性加成也被削减。
  • 主机玩家获得了一个安静但重要的生活质量提升：物品过滤器（Item Filter）支持上线，这一点直接相关，因为新的镶嵌物在你为过滤器添加对应条目之前，不会出现在旧过滤器中。本文用玩家能理解的语言解释每一次改动，并标注哪些至今仍然成立、哪些可能被后续补丁调整。
  • 本文不给出任何掉落率百分比。官方说明把这些描述为规则变化，而非公开数值，因此下面的每一处都只按方向与机制来描述。
  • 0.2.1 新增 22 个终局符文、7 个护符、15 个魂核。
  • 独特掉落不再考虑物品类别，因此戒指与护符更常见。
  • 神话独特掉落率提高；高层区域降低普通独特的占比。
  • 队伍独特性加成被削减。
  • 主机物品过滤器支持意味着新镶嵌物需要过滤器条目。

为什么物品过滤器需要新条目

[
 {

PoE2 0.1.1 Endgame: New Tower Maps, Map Boss Frequency & Arbiter of Ash Six Attempts

enpublishedverified0.1.1major-updates

Patch 0.1.1 reshaped Path of Exile 2 endgame with four new Tower Maps, a Lost Towers rework, map checkpoints, improved Citadel visibility, and the Arbiter of Ash gaining up to six attempts.

What 0.1.1 changed at a glance

  • Patch 0.1.1 was one of the first endgame-focused updates of Path of Exile 2 Early Access, and it reworked how the Atlas of Worlds felt to play. Before this patch, the pinnacle boss Arbiter of Ash allowed only a single attempt per encounter, so every death ended the run and every mistake carried an outsized cost. The update eased that pressure and simultaneously gave players more mapping infrastructure to work with.
  • This article is a historical record of the mapping and Arbiter changes that 0.1.1 introduced. It covers the four newly added Tower Maps (Alpine Ridge, Bluff, Mesa, and Sinking Spire), the reworked Lost Towers, the introduction of map checkpoints, the improved proximity and visibility of Citadels, the Overseer's Precursor Tablet and its boss-injection behaviour, and the headline change: the Arbiter of Ash now grants up to six attempts depending on difficulty.
  • Because these systems have continued to evolve since Early Access, we mark throughout which behaviours are confirmed for 0.1.1 and which may differ in the current 0.5.4e baseline. Treat the attempt counts, tablet mechanics, and map frequency described below as documented for their original patch rather than as a guarantee of the live state today.
  • Four Tower Maps added: Alpine Ridge, Bluff, Mesa, Sinking Spire.
  • Arbiter of Ash attempts raised from 1 to up to 6 (fewer at higher difficulty).
  • Map checkpoints, Citadel proximity/visibility, and the Overseer's Precursor Tablet introduced.

The Early Access endgame before 0.1.1

Path of Exile 2 Early Access launch window (0.1.x)

Relative to the current 0.5.4e baseline, this page records the 0.1.1 endgame changes that later patches have since iterated on.

  • When Path of Exile 2 entered Early Access, the endgame was intentionally lean. The Atlas offered a smaller set of maps, the Arbiter of Ash was a brutal single-attempt gatekeeper, and players had limited tools to recover from a bad run or to specialise a region. The 0.1.x line of patches was where Grinding Gear Games began filling those gaps.
  • 0.1.1 sits in that early iteration phase. It is not a large content expansion like the later Dawn of the Hunt updates; it is a targeted quality-of-life and endgame-depth pass. Reading it today, the most striking difference is how forgiving the attempt system became compared with the launch-week Arbiter experience.
  • Early Access endgame launched with a single-attempt Arbiter and a thin Tower Map pool.
  • 0.1.1 is an iterative depth pass, not a content expansion.

0.1.0
0.1.1

Map checkpoints

  • 0.1.1 introduced map checkpoints, which give players a way to regroup inside a map after death without having to fully restart the layout from the beginning. This was a meaningful quality-of-life improvement for longer or more dangerous maps, where a single death previously meant re-clearing everything.
  • Checkpoints pair naturally with the more forgiving attempt rules. Together they reduce the all-or-nothing feeling of Early Access mapping and make it easier to learn a map's mechanics through repetition rather than through a single high-stakes run.
  • Checkpoints let you resume inside a map after death.
  • They reduce the cost of learning dangerous layouts through repetition.

Citadel proximity and visibility

  • The patch also improved how Citadels appear on the Atlas. Citadels were moved closer to the Ziggurat Refuge and their visibility was improved, making it easier to find and plan routes toward these important landmarks.
  • Better Citadel visibility meant less time spent scanning the fog of war and more predictable endgame routing. For players chasing specific pinnacle content, knowing where Citadels sat relative to the Refuge streamlined the whole mapping plan.
  • Citadels moved closer to the Ziggurat Refuge.
  • On-map visibility was improved for easier routing.

Where the Overseer's Precursor Tablet came from

0.1.1

tablets

  • The Overseer's Precursor Tablet was introduced in 0.1.1 as a new atlas tablet. Its original purpose was to add a Boss to the maps that fell within its range, letting players steer their atlas toward specific boss encounters rather than leaving them to chance.
  • This is the first appearance of the boss-injection tablet mechanic in Path of Exile 2. It laid the groundwork for the later tablet ecosystems that deeper patches expanded, but in 0.1.1 its role was specifically about injecting bosses into in-range maps.
  • Introduced in 0.1.1 as an atlas tablet.
  • Original use: add a Boss to maps within the tablet's range.

Map specialisation and boss frequency

  • With the new Tower Maps and tablets, 0.1.1 made map specialisation more meaningful. One notable behaviour was that a Boss appeared roughly every four maps when specialising a region, giving players a steadier cadence of pinnacle content than the earlier sparse distribution.
  • It is important to understand that this 'about every four maps' figure was never a guaranteed floor. It describes an average cadence, not a promise that the fifth map, or any specific map, will contain a boss. Players who treated it as a hard guarantee were sometimes surprised when a boss skipped a region.
  • Combined with the Overseer's Precursor Tablet, specialisation let engaged players bias their atlas toward the bosses they wanted, but the underlying frequency remained probabilistic rather than contractual.
  • A Boss appeared roughly every 4 maps when specialising (not a guaranteed floor).
  • Frequency was probabilistic, not a hard promise per map.

流放之路2 0.1.1 终局：新塔地图、地图Boss频率与灰烬仲裁者六次尝试

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.1major-updates

0.1.1 版本重做了流放之路2 的终局：新增四张塔地图、重做失落之塔、加入地图检查点、改善城堡可见性，并让灰烬仲裁者的尝试次数提升到最多六次。

0.1.1 改了什么（速览）

  • 0.1.1 是流放之路2 抢先体验（Early Access）早期一次以终局为核心的更新，它重做了世界之石（Atlas of Worlds）的整体手感。在该版本之前，巅峰Boss 灰烬仲裁者（Arbiter of Ash）每次遭遇只允许一次尝试，因此每一次死亡都会直接结束整轮挑战，任何失误的代价都被无限放大。这次更新减轻了这种压力，同时也为玩家提供了更多地图层面的基础设施。
  • 本文是对 0.1.1 引入的地图与仲裁者改动的历史记录。它涵盖四张新增的塔地图（Alpine Ridge、Bluff、Mesa 与 Sinking Spire）、重做后的失落之塔（Lost Towers）、地图检查点（map checkpoints）的引入、城堡（Citadel）在距离与可见性上的改善、监管者先驱碑（Overseer's Precursor Tablet）及其注入Boss 的机制，以及最重磅的改动：灰烬仲裁者的尝试次数提升到最多六次（随难度变化）。
  • 由于这些系统从抢先体验以来持续演进，我们在文中会标注哪些行为是针对 0.1.1 确认的、哪些在当前 0.5.4e 基线中可能已经不同。请将下文描述的尝试次数、碑文机制与地图频率视为对应原始版本的记录，而非对今日实际状态的保证。
  • 新增四张塔地图：Alpine Ridge、Bluff、Mesa、Sinking Spire。
  • 灰烬仲裁者尝试次数从 1 次提升到最多 6 次（高难度下更少）。
  • 引入地图检查点、城堡距离/可见性优化，以及监管者先驱碑。

0.1.1 之前的抢先体验终局

流放之路2 抢先体验启动阶段（0.1.x）

相对于当前的 0.5.4e 基线，本页记录的是后续版本又迭代过的 0.1.1 终局改动。

  • 流放之路2 进入抢先体验时，终局内容是有意做得精简的。当时的世界之石地图池更小，灰烬仲裁者是一个冷酷的“单次尝试”守门人，玩家在失误后几乎没有什么恢复手段，也缺少对某个区域做专精（specialise）的工具。0.1.x 这一批补丁正是 Grinding Gear Games 开始填补这些空缺的阶段。
  • 0.1.1 处在这一早期迭代阶段。它并不像后来“猎捕黎明（Dawn of the Hunt）”那样是一次大规模内容扩张，而是一轮有针对性的生活质量（QoL）与终局深度补丁。以今天的视角回看，最令人惊讶的差别在于：相比启动周的仲裁者体验，尝试系统的容错度变得高出许多。
  • 抢先体验终局以“单次尝试的仲裁者”和单薄的塔地图池启动。
  • 0.1.1 是一次迭代式深度补丁，而非内容扩张。

各版本仲裁者尝试规则对照

灰烬仲裁者的尝试规则在 0.1.0 与 0.1.1 之间如何变化。

  "label": "离开区域"

0.1.0
0.1.1

地图检查点

  • 0.1.1 引入了地图检查点，让玩家在地图内死亡后能够就地重新集结，而不必从头完全重开整张地图的布局。对于更长或更危险的地图而言，这是一项有意义的生活质量改善——在此之前，一次死亡意味着要把所有内容重新清一遍。
  • 检查点与更宽容的尝试规则天然互补。二者共同削弱了抢先体验地图那种“全有或全无”的体验，让玩家更容易通过重复练习来学习一张地图的机制，而不是只能押注于单次高风险的通关。
  • 检查点让你在地图内死亡后可以从内部继续。
  • 它们降低了通过反复练习来学习危险布局的代价。

城堡的距离与可见性

  • 该补丁还改善了城堡在世界之石上的呈现方式。城堡被移到更靠近金字形避难所（Ziggurat Refuge）的位置，并提升了其可见性，使玩家更容易找到这些重要地标并规划前往路线。
  • 更好的城堡可见性意味着玩家花在扫视战争迷雾上的时间更少，终局路线也更可预测。对于追逐特定巅峰内容的玩家来说，清楚城堡相对于避难所的位置，让整套地图规划都更顺畅。
  • 城堡被移到更靠近金字形避难所的位置。
  • 地图上的可见性得到提升，便于路线规划。

监管者先驱碑的起源

0.1.1

tablets

  • 监管者先驱碑在 0.1.1 作为新型世界之石碑文被引入。它的原始用途是：为作用范围内的地图添加一个Boss，让玩家能够把世界之石导向特定的Boss 遭遇，而非听天由命。
  • 这是流放之路2 中“注入Boss 的碑文”机制的首次登场。它为后来更深版本所扩展的碑文生态奠定了基础，但在 0.1.1 中，它的角色明确就是向作用范围内的地图注入Boss。
  • 在 0.1.1 作为世界之石碑文被引入。
  • 原始用途：为作用范围内的地图添加一个Boss。

地图专精与Boss 频率

  • 伴随新的塔地图与碑文，0.1.1 让地图专精变得更有意义。一个值得注意的现象是：在对某个区域做专精时，大约每四张地图会出现一个Boss，相比此前稀疏的分布，玩家获得巅峰内容的节奏更稳定。
  • 需要特别理解的是，“大约每四张地图”从来都不是一个保底下限。它描述的是一种平均节奏，而非承诺第五张地图、或任何特定的一张地图必定包含Boss。把它当作硬性保证的玩家，偶尔会因为某个区域跳过了Boss 而感到意外。
  • 结合监管者先驱碑，专精让有投入的玩家能够把世界之石偏向他们想要的Boss，但底层的出现频率始终是概率性的，而非契约式的保证。
  • 专精时大约每 4 张地图出现一个Boss（并非保底下限）。
  • 频率是概率性的，而非对每张地图的硬性承诺。

PoE2 0.1.1c: Xesht, Olroth, King in the Mists Respawn Rules & /ResetAtlas Recovery

enpublishedverified0.1.1cbug-fixes

Patch 0.1.1c extended pinnacle boss respawn rules to Xesht, Olroth, and King in the Mists, and added the /ResetAtlas recovery command for bricked Atlas maps.

Quick answer

  • Patch 0.1.1c is a historical Early Access hotfix-pass that extended pinnacle boss respawn rules beyond the Arbiter of Ash. Xesht, Olroth, and King in the Mists all gained the ability to respawn after death, so a single mistake no longer ended the encounter permanently.
  • The patch also added the /ResetAtlas command. Its purpose is narrow: it is a recovery tool for an Atlas that has become fully bricked, meaning regions that can no longer be progressed or completed through normal play. It is not a normal reset and it is not a farming tool.
  • Finally, 0.1.1c added minimap icons for league mechanics and Strongboxes and reinforced map checkpoints. These changes make the post-death recovery loop and on-map navigation clearer, and they pair with the new respawn rules.
  • Xesht, Olroth, and King in the Mists gained respawn after 0.1.1c.
  • /ResetAtlas is only for fully bricked Atlas regions and does not wipe your progress.
  • Minimap icons for league mechanics and Strongboxes were added.

Version facts around 0.1.1c

Path of Exile 2 Early Access, post-0.1.1 hotfix window

Relative to the current 0.5.4e baseline, this page records the 0.1.1c respawn and Atlas recovery changes that later patches have iterated on.

  • After 0.1.1 introduced up to six Arbiter of Ash attempts, players still hit a wall with the other pinnacle bosses. Xesht, Olroth, and King in the Mists did not yet share that forgiving respawn behaviour, so a death there was often a dead end until 0.1.1c.
  • 0.1.1c is a targeted follow-up. It is not a content expansion; it is a quality-of-life and recovery pass that closes the respawn gap for the remaining pinnacle bosses and gives the developers a sanctioned way to recover maps that had become uncompletable.
  • Pre-0.1.1c, only the Arbiter of Ash had a documented multi-attempt respawn.
  • 0.1.1c extended respawn to Xesht, Olroth, and King in the Mists.

Xesht respawn flow

  • For Xesht, We That Are One, the respawn flow is tied to the Twisted Domain. Where you die inside the Twisted Domain determines where you respawn, so positioning matters even after a failed attempt.
  • One important detail: repeating the Twisted Domain for additional clears grants no experience. The respawn exists to give you another shot at the boss, not to farm endless XP from the same encounter.
  • Respawn position is set by your death location inside the Twisted Domain.
  • Repeating the domain grants no XP.

Olroth respawn flow

  • Olroth, Origin of the Fall, is reached through an Expedition Logbook, and its respawn follows that same entry item. The Crux of Nothingness is the relevant Expedition anchor for the encounter.
  • Because Olroth is gated behind the Expedition Logbook system, the respawn is owned by the logbook holder. Losing the logbook owner's access is the main way the respawn chain breaks.
  • Olroth is entered via an Expedition Logbook.
  • The Crux of Nothingness is the relevant Expedition anchor.

King in the Mists respawn flow

  • King in the Mists, the pinnacle version, is reached through the Audience with the King. Its respawn follows that entry item, so the Audience owner controls whether the party can re-enter.
  • As with the other pinnacle bosses, the respawn is keyed to the owner of the entry item rather than to every individual player, which is what makes the party-trigger rules below matter.
  • King in the Mists is entered via the Audience with the King.
  • The Audience owner controls re-entry.

Party trigger rules

  • A consistent rule across these bosses is that the owner of the entry item triggers the respawn for the whole party. For Xesht that is the Breachstone owner, for Olroth the Logbook owner, and for King in the Mists the Audience owner.
  • This means only the owner can click respawn after a death; the rest of the party rides along on the owner's action. If the owner leaves or loses the item, the group's ability to respawn collapses even if other members are still present.
  • The entry-item owner triggers the whole-party respawn.
  • Only the owner can click respawn after death.

Atlas recovery with /ResetAtlas

  • The /ResetAtlas command is a recovery tool for an Atlas that has become fully bricked. A bricked Atlas means a region that can no longer be progressed or completed through normal play, leaving the player stuck with no valid path forward.
  • Crucially, /ResetAtlas does not wipe your progress. It is not a normal reset command and it is not a farming tool; it exists only to un-brick maps that are genuinely stuck, preserving the work you have already done elsewhere in the Atlas.
  • Use it sparingly and only when a region is truly unrecoverable. Because it is a recovery measure rather than a routine tool, treating it like a map reroll would be a misunderstanding of its purpose.
  • /ResetAtlas is only for fully bricked Atlas regions.
  • It does NOT wipe your progress and is not a farming tool.

流放之路2 0.1.1c：Xesht、Olroth、迷雾之王的重试规则与 /ResetAtlas 恢复

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.1cbug-fixes

0.1.1c 将巅峰Boss 重试规则扩展到 Xesht、Olroth 与迷雾之王，并新增 /ResetAtlas 用于修复彻底损坏的 Atlas 区域。

一句话答案

  • 0.1.1c 是抢先体验时期一次历史性的热修补丁，把巅峰Boss 的重试规则从灰烬仲裁者扩展到了其他Boss。Xesht、Olroth 与迷雾之王（King in the Mists）都获得了死亡后重试的能力，因此一次失误不再意味着遭遇的永久终结。
  • 该补丁还新增了 /ResetAtlas 命令。它的用途非常窄：这是一个用于“彻底损坏（bricked）”的 Atlas 的恢复工具，即那些已经无法通过正常玩法推进或完成的区域。它不是普通的重置命令，也不是刷图工具。
  • 最后，0.1.1c 为联盟机制与坚固箱（Strongbox）添加了小地图图标，并强化了地图检查点。这些改动让死亡后的恢复循环与地图内的导航更清晰，也与新的重试规则相辅相成。
  • 0.1.1c 之后，Xesht、Olroth 与迷雾之王都获得了重试能力。
  • /ResetAtlas 仅用于彻底损坏的 Atlas 区域，且不会清除你的进度。
  • 为联盟机制与坚固箱新增了小地图图标。

0.1.1c 相关的版本事实

流放之路2 抢先体验，0.1.1 之后的热修窗口

相对于当前的 0.5.4e 基线，本页记录的是后续版本又迭代过的 0.1.1c 重试与 Atlas 恢复改动。

  • 在 0.1.1 引入灰烬仲裁者最多六次尝试之后，玩家在其他巅峰Boss 上仍然碰壁。Xesht、Olroth 与迷雾之王尚未共享这种宽容的重试行为，因此在那里死亡往往是一条死路，直到 0.1.1c 出现。
  • 0.1.1c 是一次有针对性的后续补丁。它不是内容扩张，而是一轮生活质量与恢复补丁，弥合了剩余巅峰Boss 的重试缺口，并为开发者提供了一种官方认可的方式来恢复那些已经无法完成的地图。
  • 在 0.1.1c 之前，只有灰烬仲裁者拥有文档化的多次尝试重试。
  • 0.1.1c 将重试扩展到了 Xesht、Olroth 与迷雾之王。

Boss 尝试次数演变：0.1.1 到 0.1.1c

 }

Xesht 的重试流程

  • 对于 Xesht（We That Are One），重试流程与扭曲领域（Twisted Domain）绑定。你在扭曲领域内死亡的地点，决定了你重生的位置，因此即使在一次失败的尝试之后，站位依然重要。
  • 一个重要细节：重复进入扭曲领域再次击杀不会获得经验。重试存在的意义是给你另一次挑战Boss 的机会，而非让你从同一场遭遇中刷取无限经验。
  • 重生位置由你在扭曲领域内的死亡位置决定。
  • 重复进入该领域不会获得经验。

Olroth 的重试流程

  • Olroth（Origin of the Fall）是通过远征日志（Expedition Logbook）进入的，其重试也遵循同一个进入道具。虚空之枢（Crux of Nothingness）是这场遭遇相关的远征锚点。
  • 由于 Olroth 被远征日志系统所门控，重试归属于日志的持有者。失去日志持有者的访问权，是重试链断裂的主要原因。
  • Olroth 通过远征日志进入。
  • 虚空之枢是相关的远征锚点。

迷雾之王的重试流程

  • 巅峰版本的迷雾之王（King in the Mists）是通过“觐见迷雾之王（Audience with the King）”进入的。它的重试也遵循该进入道具，因此觐见的持有者掌控队伍能否重新进入。
  • 与其他巅峰Boss 一样，重试绑定于进入道具的持有者，而非每一个单独的玩家，这也正是下方队伍触发规则之所以重要的原因。
  • 迷雾之王通过“觐见迷雾之王”进入。
  • 觐见的持有者掌控重新进入。

队伍触发规则

  • 这些Boss 之间一致的规则是：进入道具的持有者会为整个队伍触发重试。对于 Xesht 是裂界石（Breachstone）持有者，对于 Olroth 是日志持有者，对于迷雾之王则是觐见持有者。
  • 这意味着死亡后只有持有者能点击重试；队伍其余成员搭的是持有者的“顺风车”。如果持有者离开或丢失了道具，即便其他成员仍在场，整队的重生能力也会崩溃。
  • 进入道具的持有者为全队触发重试。
  • 死亡后只有持有者能点击重试。

用 /ResetAtlas 恢复 Atlas

  • /ResetAtlas 命令是用于“彻底损坏”的 Atlas 的恢复工具。所谓损坏的 Atlas，指的是一个已经无法通过正常玩法推进或完成的区域，让玩家陷入无路可走的境地。
  • 关键点是：/ResetAtlas 不会清除你的进度。它不是普通的重置命令，也不是刷图工具；它只用于解除那些真正卡死、无法推进的地图，并保留你已在 Atlas 其他地方完成的工作。
  • 请谨慎使用，只有在某个区域确实无法恢复时才动用。因为它是一种恢复手段而非日常工具，把它当成地图重投（reroll）来用是对其用途的误解。
  • /ResetAtlas 仅用于彻底损坏的 Atlas 区域。
  • 它不会清除你的进度，也不是刷图工具。

[
 {

Path of Exile 2 0.1.0 Early Access Launch: 6 Classes, 12 Ascendancies, 3 Acts and How It Differs From Current

enpublishedverified0.1.0major-updates

Path of Exile 2 entered Early Access on 2024-12-06 with six base classes, twelve ascendancies, a three-act campaign and a standalone endgame. This page explains exactly what 0.1.0 shipped, why Day-1 builds no longer match the current client, and which systems were later replaced by 0.2 to 0.5.

Quick summary

  • Path of Exile 2 launched in Early Access on 6 December 2024 with client version 0.1.0. It opened with six base classes, twelve ascendancies, a three-act campaign and a standalone endgame built around the Atlas of Worlds. For players coming from Path of Exile 1, this was a new game with new systems rather than an extension of the old one.
  • The launch was deliberately a foundation. Within the first three days the team was already adjusting clearly overtuned skills such as Supercharged Slam, Magnetic Salvo and the Gas Cloud interactions, which set the tone for a launch week full of rapid hotfixes.
  • This page is the total-entry history for 0.1.0: what shipped at launch, how the first week stabilised, and which parts of 0.1.0 are still recognisable in client 0.5.4e versus what later patches rewrote. Anything we could not confirm against the current client is flagged as unknown rather than guessed.
  • Early Access began 2024-12-06 on client 0.1.0
  • Six base classes and twelve ascendancies at launch
  • Three-act campaign plus a standalone Atlas endgame
  • First-week hotfixes already tuned Supercharged Slam, Magnetic Salvo and Gas Cloud

Historical warning

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.1.0 era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • 0.1.0 is a historical baseline, not a current玩法 guide. The classes, ascendancies and endgame systems it introduced were the starting point, and several were expanded or replaced within the first year of Early Access.
  • Reading a Day-1 build video today is misleading: the skill numbers, ascendancy nodes and Atlas rules it shows were changed by launch-week hotfixes and then again by 0.2, 0.3, 0.4 and 0.5. Treat 0.1.0 footage as a record of the launch state, not as a current tutorial.
  • 0.1.0 = system starting point, not final design
  • Day-1 build videos are historical records, not current guides
  • All current-rule claims below are checked against 0.5.4e

What Early Access actually shipped

  • The launch confirmed the six base classes players still recognise: Sorceress, Warrior, Ranger, Witch, Mercenary and Monk. Each launched with two ascendancies, giving twelve total at 0.1.0 before any later class was added.
  • The campaign covered three acts and led into a separate endgame. That endgame already had its own Atlas, maps, league mechanics and pinnacle structure, so 0.1.0 was a complete loop rather than a vertical slice.
  • Sorceress, Warrior, Ranger, Witch, Mercenary, Monk
  • Twelve ascendancies (two per class)
  • Three-act campaign into a standalone Atlas endgame

Patch family timeline

0.1.02024-12-06Early Access LaunchSix classes, twelve ascendancies, three-act campaign, standalone Atlas endgame, original league mechanics and pinnacle路线.
0.1.0b2024-12-09HotfixLowered Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage; fixed loading, Atlas Controller Refund, Console Gateway and crashes.
0.1.0c2024-12-13Economy PatchFirst loot and currency rebalance: Regal and Gemcutter's Prism rates up, Chaos and Artificer redistribution, Waystone prefix buff.
0.1.0d2024-12-17Mechanics PatchTrigger Gem Energy rework (Monster Power and Ailment Strength), Leech buff, Block and Dodge changes, Trial Key clarity.
0.1.0e2024-12-20StabilisationCheckpoint fast travel, Respec cost cut, Honour close-range fix, large skill and support overhaul.
0.2.0+2025-04-04Later lineThe current major line (baseline 0.5.4e) that rewrote large parts of the 0.1.0 foundation.

Launch volatility: Day-1 vs current

Launch-day 0.1.0 (December 2024)

Several skills shipped clearly overtuned. Supercharged Slam, Magnetic Salvo and the Gas Grenade / Gas Arrow / Flame Wall ignite loops dominated early footage, and some Day-1 builds looked dramatically stronger than they would within days.

Stabilised 0.1.0 (after first-week hotfixes)

0.1.0b and the surrounding hotfixes pulled Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage down, and the team kept watching clear outliers. By 0.2 to 0.5 the numbers had moved again, so the current client bears little resemblance to launch balance.

流放之路2 0.1.0 抢先体验首发：6 个职业、12 个升华与和当前版本的差异

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.0major-updates

流放之路2 于 2024-12-06 以客户端 0.1.0 进入抢先体验，首发包含 6 个基础职业、12 个升华、三幕战役以及独立的终局。本页说明 0.1.0 到底发布了什么、为什么首日 Build 与当前客户端不再一致，以及哪些系统在 0.2 至 0.5 中被替换。

快速结论

  • 流放之路2 于 2024 年 12 月 6 日以客户端 0.1.0 进入抢先体验。首发包含六个基础职业、十二个升华、三幕战役，以及围绕 Atlas of Worlds 的独立终局。对于来自流放之路1 的玩家，这是一个拥有全新机制的新游戏，而非旧作的延伸。
  • 首发被刻意定位为地基。在最初三天内，团队就已经在调整明显失衡的技能，例如 Supercharged Slam、Magnetic Salvo 以及 Gas Cloud 联动，这也奠定了首周大量热修的基调。
  • 本页是 0.1.0 的总入口：首发发布了什么、首周如何稳定、以及哪些 0.1.0 部分在客户端 0.5.4e 中仍可辨认、哪些被后续版本重写。任何无法对照当前客户端确认的内容都会被标记为未知，而非猜测。
  • 抢先体验于 2024-12-06 以 0.1.0 客户端开启
  • 首发六个基础职业、十二个升华
  • 三幕战役加独立 Atlas 终局
  • 首周热修已调整 Supercharged Slam、Magnetic Salvo 与 Gas Cloud

历史说明

流放之路2 抢先体验 — 0.1.0 时代

本页关于当前状态的结论均以客户端 0.5.4e 为对照基线。

  • 0.1.0 是历史基线，而非当前玩法攻略。它引入的职业、升华与终局系统只是起点，其中多个在抢先体验首年内被扩展或替换。
  • 今天去看首日的 Build 视频会产生误导：视频中的技能数值、升华节点与 Atlas 规则，已经被首周热修以及 0.2、0.3、0.4、0.5 再次改动。请将 0.1.0 录像视为首发状态的记录，而非当前教程。
  • 0.1.0 = 系统起点，而非最终设计
  • 首日 Build 视频是历史记录，不是当前攻略
  • 以下所有当前规则结论均对照 0.5.4e 核验

抢先体验到底发布了什么

  • 首发确认了玩家至今仍熟悉的六个基础职业：Sorceress、Warrior、Ranger、Witch、Mercenary 与 Monk。每个职业首发即带两个升华，因此 0.1.0 时共有十二个升华，之后才加入新职业。
  • 战役覆盖三幕，并通向独立的终局。该终局当时已拥有自己的 Atlas、地图、联盟机制与巅峰（Pinnacle）结构，因此 0.1.0 是一个完整的闭环，而非纵向切片。
  • Sorceress、Warrior、Ranger、Witch、Mercenary、Monk
  • 十二个升华（每职业两个）
  • 三幕战役进入独立 Atlas 终局

版本家族时间线

0.1.02024-12-06抢先体验首发六个职业、十二升华、三幕战役、独立 Atlas 终局、原始联盟机制与巅峰路线。
0.1.0b2024-12-09热修降低 Gas Grenade 与 Gas Arrow 的爆炸伤害；修复加载、Atlas Controller Refund、Console Gateway 与崩溃。
0.1.0c2024-12-13经济补丁首轮掉落与货币重平衡：Regal 与 Gemcutter's Prism 掉率提升，Chaos 与 Artificer 重新分配，Waystone 前缀增强。
0.1.0d2024-12-17机制补丁Trigger Gem Energy 重做（引入 Monster Power 与 Ailment Strength）、Leech 增强、Block 与 Dodge 改动、Trial Key 清晰度提升。
0.1.0e2024-12-20稳定化Checkpoint 快速传送、Respec 成本下降、Honour 近战修正、大规模技能与辅助重做。
0.2.0+2025-04-04后续线当前主要版本线（基线 0.5.4e），重写了 0.1.0 地基的大部分内容。

首发波动：首日 vs 当前

首发日 0.1.0（2024 年 12 月）

若干技能首发时明显失衡。Supercharged Slam、Magnetic Salvo 以及 Gas Grenade / Gas Arrow / Flame Wall 的点燃联动主导了早期录像，部分首日 Build 在几天内看起来明显强于实际。

稳定后的 0.1.0（首周热修后）

0.1.0b 及后续热修下调了 Gas Grenade 与 Gas Arrow 的爆炸伤害，团队持续监控明显离群者。到 0.2 至 0.5 时数值再次变动，因此当前客户端与首发平衡已大不相同。

Path of Exile 2 0.1.0 Atlas & Endgame Baseline: How the Original Maps, Waystones and League Mechanics Worked

enpublishedverified0.1.0major-updates

At 0.1.0 the endgame was a standalone Atlas of Worlds with Waystones, Towers, Tablets and Citadels, plus Breach, Expedition, Ritual and Delirium in maps. This page records exactly what the original endgame shipped with and which parts were rewritten by later patches.

Quick summary

  • Path of Exile 2 launched its endgame at 0.1.0 as a standalone Atlas of Worlds. Rather than a single map pool, the launch endgame combined Waystones that opened map nodes, Towers that projected buffs across regions, Tablets that modified those towers, and Citadels that anchored the late map ring.
  • On top of the Atlas, four league mechanics — Breach, Expedition, Ritual and Delirium — were present in maps from day one. This page is the total-entry history of that original endgame: what 0.1.0 shipped, how it was stabilised in the first weeks, and which systems still resemble the current client versus what later patches rebuilt.
  • Standalone Atlas of Worlds at launch with Waystones, Towers, Tablets, Citadels
  • Breach, Expedition, Ritual and Delirium active in maps from day one
  • First weeks tuned Waystone prefixes and map sustain before larger rebuilds

Historical warning

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.1.0 endgame era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • 0.1.0 is a historical baseline, not a current endgame guide. The Atlas and its supporting systems were expanded and rebuilt repeatedly across the first year of Early Access.
  • Treating a 0.1.0 Atlas video as a current tutorial is misleading: Waystone tiering, Tower behaviour and map sustain were changed within the first weeks and then again by 0.2, 0.3, 0.4 and 0.5.
  • 0.1.0 = endgame starting point, not final design
  • Day-1 Atlas videos are historical records, not current guides
  • All current-rule claims below are checked against 0.5.4e

What the 0.1.0 Atlas contained

  • The launch Atlas presented a ring of map nodes that players progressed through using Waystones. Towers sat on the overworld map and, once captured, projected a modifier across nearby map nodes; Tablets slotted into Towers to change that modifier, and Citadels appeared in the outer ring as high-threat encounters.
  • This structure let a single region feel different from the next, but the exact rules for Waystone rarity, Tower coverage and Tablet effects were in their first form and changed soon after.
  • Waystones open map nodes on the Atlas ring
  • Towers project modifiers; Tablets change those modifiers
  • Citadels anchored the outer high-threat ring

Patch family timeline

0.1.02024-12-06Early Access LaunchStandalone Atlas with Waystones, Towers, Tablets and Citadels; Breach, Expedition, Ritual, Delirium in maps.
0.1.0c2024-12-13Economy PatchWaystone prefix buff and currency redistribution improved early map sustain.
0.1.0e2024-12-20StabilisationCheckpoint fast travel and respec cost cuts eased endgame pacing.
0.2.0+2025-04-04Later lineThe current major line (baseline 0.5.4e) that rebuilt large parts of the 0.1.0 Atlas.

Launch Atlas vs current

Launch-day 0.1.0 Atlas (December 2024)

Waystones, Towers, Tablets and Citadels worked in their first form. Map sustain and Waystone rarity were rough, and early pinnacle access was costly to retry.

Current Atlas (client 0.5.4e)

The Atlas was rebuilt through 0.2 to 0.5: Waystone rules, Tower coverage and Citadel recovery all differ from launch. Old footage and guides no longer describe the current map.

流放之路 2 0.1.0 万象终界与终局基线:原始地图、界石与异界机制如何运作

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.0major-updates

0.1.0 的终局是一套独立的万象终界,包含界石、尖塔、石板与城邦,地图中还有裂界、远征、仪式与迷妄。本页记录原始终局究竟包含什么,以及哪些部分被后续补丁重写。

快速概览

  • 流放之路 2 在 0.1.0 以一套独立的万象终界开启终局。首发终局并非单一地图池,而是结合了开启地图节点的界石、向区域投射增益的尖塔、改造尖塔的石板,以及锚定后期地图环的城邦。
  • 在万象终界之上,裂界、远征、仪式、迷妄四种异界机制从第一天起就存在于地图中。本页是这套原始终局的完整历史:0.1.0 发布了什么、首周如何稳定,以及哪些系统仍与当前客户端相似、哪些被后续补丁重建。
  • 首发即独立的万象终界,包含界石、尖塔、石板、城邦
  • 裂界、远征、仪式、迷妄从首日即活跃于地图
  • 首周调整了界石前缀与地图续航,之后才是更大规模的重建

历史提醒

流放之路 2 抢先体验 — 0.1.0 终局时代

本页当前状态描述均以客户端 0.5.4e 为基准核对。

  • 0.1.0 是历史基线,而非当前终局指南。万象终界及其支撑系统在抢先体验的第一年里被反复扩展与重建。
  • 把 0.1.0 的终界视频当作当前教程会误导人:界石分级、尖塔行为与地图续航在首周就被改动,之后又被 0.2、0.3、0.4、0.5 再次修改。
  • 0.1.0 = 终局起点,而非最终设计
  • 首发终界视频是历史记录,不是当前指南
  • 下列所有当前规则描述均以 0.5.4e 核对

0.1.0 万象终界包含什么

  • 首发万象终界呈现一圈地图节点,玩家使用界石向前推进。尖塔位于大地图之上,占领后会向附近地图节点投射修正;石板插入尖塔以改变该修正;城邦出现在外圈,作为高威胁遭遇。
  • 这套结构让不同区域各有特色,但界石稀有度、尖塔覆盖范围与石板效果的精确规则都处于最初形态,不久后便被改动。
  • 界石开启万象终界上的地图节点
  • 尖塔投射修正,石板改变这些修正
  • 城邦锚定外圈高威胁区域

版本族时间线

0.1.02024-12-06抢先体验首发独立的万象终界,含界石、尖塔、石板、城邦;地图中已有裂界、远征、仪式、迷妄。
0.1.0c2024-12-13经济补丁界石前缀增强与通货再分配改善了早期地图续航。
0.1.0e2024-12-20稳定补丁检查点快速传送与洗点成本下调改善了终局节奏。
0.2.0+2025-04-04后续主线当前主线(基线 0.5.4e),重建了 0.1.0 万象终界的众多部分。

首发终界 vs 当前

首发当日 0.1.0 终界(2024 年 12 月)

界石、尖塔、石板、城邦都处于最初形态。地图续航与界石稀有度尚粗糙,早期巅峰进入的重试成本很高。

当前终界(客户端 0.5.4e)

万象终界在 0.2 至 0.5 间被重建:界石规则、尖塔覆盖范围与城邦恢复均不同于首发。旧的视频与指南已不再描述当前地图。

Path of Exile 2 0.1.0 Ascendancy Trials Baseline: Trial of Sekhemas & Trial of Chaos at Launch

enpublishedverified0.1.0major-updates

At 0.1.0, ascendancies were earned by surviving the Trial of Sekhemas (a Honour-based gauntlet) and the Trial of Chaos. This page records exactly how the original trial system worked and how later patches changed it.

Quick summary

  • In 0.1.0, reaching an ascendancy meant surviving one of two trial types. The Trial of Sekhemas was a multi-room gauntlet tracked by a Honour resource that drained as you took damage; the Trial of Chaos was a wave-based challenge with random房间 modifiers.
  • Both trials dropped from specific content and granted ascendancy points on completion. This page is the total-entry history of that original trial system: what 0.1.0 shipped, how the first weeks adjusted it, and which trial rules still resemble the current client versus what later patches rebuilt.
  • Two trial paths: Trial of Sekhemas (Honour) and Trial of Chaos (waves)
  • Trials granted ascendancy points on completion
  • First adjustments arrived in 0.1.0d (trial key clarity) and 0.1.0e (respec cost)

Historical warning

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.1.0 trial era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • 0.1.0 is a historical baseline, not a current trial guide. The Honour mechanic and Chaos room rules were adjusted within the first weeks and then again by 0.2, 0.3, 0.4 and 0.5.
  • Watching a Day-1 trial run today is misleading: the Honour drain, room modifiers and reward steps it shows were changed by launch-week hotfixes and later patches. Treat 0.1.0 footage as a record of the launch state.
  • 0.1.0 = trial system starting point, not final design
  • Day-1 trial runs are historical records, not current guides
  • All current-rule claims below are checked against 0.5.4e

What the 0.1.0 trial system contained

  • The Trial of Sekhemas opened as a sequence of rooms where a Honour bar represented your remaining life for the whole run; losing all Honour failed the trial regardless of character health. The Trial of Chaos presented waves with random room modifiers that escalated difficulty.
  • Each trial type granted a different flavour of ascendancy progression, and both were required across a character's career to reach full ascendancy. The exact key drops and room sets were in their first form and changed soon after.
  • Sekhemas: Honour bar tracks the whole run
  • Chaos: wave-based with random room modifiers
  • Both trials fed ascendancy progression

Patch family timeline

0.1.02024-12-06Early Access LaunchTrial of Sekhemas (Honour) and Trial of Chaos launched as the two ascendancy paths.
0.1.0d2024-12-17Mechanics PatchTrial Key clarity improved how players found and understood trial access.
0.1.0e2024-12-20StabilisationRespec cost cut eased the pressure of committing to an ascendancy.
0.2.0+2025-04-04Later lineThe current major line (baseline 0.5.4e) that reworked trial rooms and rewards.

Launch trials vs current

Launch-day 0.1.0 trials (December 2024)

The Honour gauntlet and Chaos waves worked in their first form. Close-range Honour drains felt harsh and trial key access was unclear.

Current trials (client 0.5.4e)

Trial rooms and rewards were reworked through 0.2 to 0.5, Honour drains were fixed, and key access is clearer. Old footage no longer describes the current trials.

流放之路 2 0.1.0 升华试炼基线:首发时的赛克哈玛斯试炼与混沌试炼

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.0major-updates

0.1.0 中,升华通过幸存于赛克哈玛斯试炼(基于荣誉的闯关)与混沌试炼获得。本页记录原始试炼系统究竟如何运作,以及后续补丁如何改动它。

快速概览

  • 在 0.1.0 中,获得升华需要幸存于两类试炼之一。赛克哈玛斯试炼是多房间闯关,由荣誉资源追踪,受击时荣誉流失;混沌试炼是基于波次、带随机房间修正的挑战。
  • 两类试炼由特定内容掉落,完成后给予升华点。本页是这套原始试炼系统的完整历史:0.1.0 发布了什么、首周如何调整,以及哪些试炼规则仍与当前客户端相似、哪些被后续补丁重建。
  • 两条试炼路径:赛克哈玛斯试炼(荣誉)与混沌试炼(波次)
  • 试炼完成后给予升华点
  • 首次调整出现在 0.1.0d(试炼钥匙说明)与 0.1.0e(洗点成本)

历史提醒

流放之路 2 抢先体验 — 0.1.0 试炼时代

本页当前状态描述均以客户端 0.5.4e 为基准核对。

  • 0.1.0 是历史基线,而非当前试炼指南。荣誉机制与混沌房间规则在首周被调整,之后又被 0.2、0.3、0.4、0.5 修改。
  • 今天观看首日的试炼通关会误导人:它所展示的荣誉流失、房间修正与奖励步骤,已被首发热修与后续补丁改动。请把 0.1.0 视频视为首发状态的记录。
  • 0.1.0 = 试炼系统起点,而非最终设计
  • 首日试炼通关是历史记录,不是当前指南
  • 下列所有当前规则描述均以 0.5.4e 核对

0.1.0 试炼系统包含什么

  • 赛克哈玛斯试炼是一连串房间,荣誉条代表整次闯关的剩余生命;无论角色血量如何,荣誉归零即试炼失败。混沌试炼呈现波次,并带随机房间修正以提升难度。
  • 两类试炼提供不同风味的升华进程,角色要达成完整升华须经历两者。钥匙掉落与房间组合都处于最初形态,不久后便被改动。
  • 赛克哈玛斯:荣誉条追踪整次闯关
  • 混沌:基于波次,带随机房间修正
  • 两类试炼共同构成升华进程

版本族时间线

0.1.02024-12-06抢先体验首发赛克哈玛斯试炼(荣誉)与混沌试炼作为两条升华路径首发。
0.1.0d2024-12-17机制补丁试炼钥匙说明改善了玩家寻找与理解试炼入口的方式。
0.1.0e2024-12-20稳定补丁洗点成本下调缓解了对升华抉择的压力。
0.2.0+2025-04-04后续主线当前主线(基线 0.5.4e),重做了试炼房间与奖励。

首发试炼 vs 当前

首发当日 0.1.0 试炼(2024 年 12 月)

荣誉闯关与混沌波次为最初形态。近身荣誉流失过于严苛,试炼钥匙入口也不清晰。

当前试炼(客户端 0.5.4e)

试炼房间与奖励在 0.2 至 0.5 间被重做,荣誉流失已修复,钥匙入口更清晰。旧视频已不再描述当前试炼。

Path of Exile 2 0.1.0b Launch Hotfix: Gas Grenade Nerf, Crash Fixes & First-Week Stabilisation

enpublishedverified0.1.0bhotfixes

The 0.1.0b hotfix lowered Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage and fixed loading, Atlas Controller Refund, Console Gateway and crashes. This page records exactly what 0.1.0b changed and why Day-1 build footage no longer matches the client.

Quick summary

  • Within three days of launch, 0.1.0b arrived as the first hotfix. It pulled down Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage, which had dominated early footage, and fixed a set of loading, refund and crash problems that were blocking progression.
  • This page is the total-entry history of 0.1.0b: what it changed, why those changes mattered for Day-1 builds, and which fixes still describe the current client versus what later patches moved again. Anything we could not confirm against the current client is flagged as unknown rather than guessed.
  • 0.1.0b landed 2024-12-09, three days after launch
  • Lowered Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage
  • Fixed loading, Atlas Controller Refund, Console Gateway and crashes

Historical warning

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.1.0b hotfix era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • 0.1.0b is a historical hotfix, not a current玩法 guide. It was the first of many rapid launch-week adjustments, and the numbers it touched were changed again by 0.2 to 0.5.
  • Reading a Day-1 build video today is misleading: the Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow loops it shows were already weakened by 0.1.0b and then moved further by later balance. Treat 0.1.0b footage as a record of the launch state, not as a current tutorial.
  • 0.1.0b = first rapid adjustment, not final design
  • Day-1 build videos are historical records, not current guides
  • All current-rule claims below are checked against 0.5.4e

What 0.1.0b actually changed

  • The headline balance change was explosion damage: Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosions were dealing far more than intended, so 0.1.0b brought them down to a sane baseline. This directly affected the strong Day-1 poison and ignite loops.
  • Alongside balance, 0.1.0b fixed blocking bugs: area-loading failures, the Atlas Controller Refund not returning points correctly, the Console Gateway not functioning, and several crash paths that could end a session. These were progression-blocking, so they shipped fast.
  • Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage reduced
  • Loading failures and crash paths fixed
  • Atlas Controller Refund and Console Gateway corrected

Patch family timeline

0.1.02024-12-06Early Access LaunchSix classes, twelve ascendancies, three-act campaign, standalone Atlas endgame.
0.1.0b2024-12-09HotfixLowered Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosion damage; fixed loading, Atlas Controller Refund, Console Gateway and crashes.
0.1.0c2024-12-13Economy PatchFirst loot and currency rebalance and Waystone prefix buff.
0.2.0+2025-04-04Later lineThe current major line (baseline 0.5.4e) that moved the numbers again.

Launch Day-1 vs after 0.1.0b

Launch-day 0.1.0 (before 0.1.0b)

Gas Grenade and Gas Arrow explosions dealt far more than intended, dominating early footage. Loading failures and crashes could block progression.

After 0.1.0b

Explosion damage was pulled to a sane baseline and the blocking loading, refund, gateway and crash bugs were fixed. Later patches moved the numbers again.

流放之路 2 0.1.0b 首发热修:气爆手雷削弱、崩溃修复与首周稳定

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.0bhotfixes

0.1.0b 热修下调了气爆手雷与气爆箭的爆炸伤害,并修复了加载、万象控制器返还、控制台网关与崩溃问题。本页记录 0.1.0b 究竟改了什么,以及为何首日构筑视频已不再与客户端一致。

快速概览

  • 首发三天内,0.1.0b 作为首个热修上线。它下调了在早期视频中称霸的气爆手雷与气爆箭的爆炸伤害,并修复了一系列阻碍进度的加载、返还与崩溃问题。
  • 本页是 0.1.0b 的完整历史:它改了什么、这些改动为何对首日构筑重要,以及哪些修复仍描述当前客户端、哪些被后续补丁再次改动。任何无法对照当前客户端确认的内容都会标注为未知,而非臆测。
  • 0.1.0b 于 2024-12-09 上线,距首发仅三天
  • 下调气爆手雷与气爆箭爆炸伤害
  • 修复加载、万象控制器返还、控制台网关与崩溃

历史提醒

流放之路 2 抢先体验 — 0.1.0b 热修时代

本页当前状态描述均以客户端 0.5.4e 为基准核对。

  • 0.1.0b 是历史热修,而非当前玩法指南。它是众多快速首周调整中的第一次,它触及的数值在 0.2 至 0.5 间再次变动。
  • 今天观看首日构筑视频会误导人:它所展示的气爆手雷与气爆箭循环已在 0.1.0b 被削弱,并被后续平衡进一步改动。请把 0.1.0b 视频视为首发状态的记录,而非当前教程。
  • 0.1.0b = 首次快速调整,而非最终设计
  • 首日构筑视频是历史记录,不是当前指南
  • 下列所有当前规则描述均以 0.5.4e 核对

0.1.0b 究竟改了什么

  • 最显眼的平衡改动是爆炸伤害:气爆手雷与气爆箭造成了远超预期的伤害,因此 0.1.0b 将其下调到合理基线。这直接影响了强势的首日中毒与点燃循环。
  • 在平衡之外,0.1.0b 修复了阻碍进程的 Bug:区域加载失败、万象控制器返还未正确退还点数、控制台网关失效,以及若干会终结会话的崩溃路径。这些问题阻碍进程,因此快速上线。
  • 气爆手雷与气爆箭爆炸伤害下调
  • 加载失败与崩溃路径已修复
  • 万象控制器返还与控制台网关已修正

版本族时间线

0.1.02024-12-06抢先体验首发六职业、十二升华、三章战役、独立万象终界。
0.1.0b2024-12-09热修下调气爆手雷与气爆箭爆炸伤害;修复加载、万象控制器返还、控制台网关与崩溃。
0.1.0c2024-12-13经济补丁首次战利品与通货再平衡,以及界石前缀增强。
0.2.0+2025-04-04后续主线当前主线(基线 0.5.4e),再次改动了数值。

Path of Exile 2 0.1.0c Loot & Currency Patch: Regal/Gemcutter Rates, Chaos/Artificer Redistribution & Waystone Prefix

enpublishedverified0.1.0cbalance

The 0.1.0c economy patch raised Regal Orb and Gemcutter's Prism drop rates, redistributed Chaos and Artificer's Orbs, and buffed Waystone prefixes to improve early map sustain. This page records exactly what 0.1.0c changed and how later patches moved the economy again.

Quick summary

  • The 0.1.0c economy patch, about a week after launch, tackled the early economy. It raised the drop rate of Regal Orbs and Gemcutter's Prisms, redistributed Chaos Orbs and Artificer's Orbs to better places, and buffed Waystone prefixes so maps sustained more reliably.
  • This page is the total-entry history of 0.1.0c: what it changed in the economy, why it mattered for early progression, and which economy rules still resemble the current client versus what later patches rebuilt.
  • 0.1.0c landed 2024-12-13, one week after launch
  • Higher Regal Orb and Gemcutter's Prism drop rates
  • Chaos and Artificer redistribution plus Waystone prefix buff

Historical warning

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.1.0c economy era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • 0.1.0c is a historical economy patch, not a current economy guide. The currency rates and Waystone rules it touched were reworked repeatedly across the first year of Early Access.
  • Reading a Day-1 economy guide today is misleading: the Regal, Gemcutter, Chaos and Artificer placement it describes was changed by 0.1.0c and then again by 0.2 to 0.5. Treat 0.1.0c footage as a record of the launch economy, not as a current tutorial.
  • 0.1.0c = economy starting point, not final design
  • Day-1 economy guides are historical records, not current guides
  • All current-rule claims below are checked against 0.5.4e

What 0.1.0c actually changed

  • The headline economy change was currency availability: Regal Orbs and Gemcutter's Prisms, both gatekeepers for upgrading and qualitying gear, dropped more often so players were not hard-blocked. Chaos Orbs and Artificer's Orbs were redistributed to drop from more appropriate content.
  • On the endgame side, Waystone prefixes were buffed. Stronger prefixes meant maps were more likely to chain into further maps, improving the early sustain that 0.1.0 launch had struggled with.
  • Regal and Gemcutter drop rates up
  • Chaos and Artificer redistributed
  • Waystone prefixes buffed for sustain

Patch family timeline

0.1.02024-12-06Early Access LaunchLaunch economy with rough currency and Waystone sustain.
0.1.0b2024-12-09HotfixSkill and crash fixes; no economy change yet.
0.1.0c2024-12-13Economy PatchRegal/Gemcutter rates up, Chaos/Artificer redistribution, Waystone prefix buff.
0.2.0+2025-04-04Later lineThe current major line (baseline 0.5.4e) that reworked the economy again.

Launch economy vs after 0.1.0c

Launch-day 0.1.0 economy (before 0.1.0c)

Regal and Gemcutter drops were scarce, Chaos and Artificer placement was awkward, and Waystone prefixes rarely sustained maps.

After 0.1.0c

Regal and Gemcutter dropped more often, Chaos and Artificer moved to better content, and Waystone prefixes buffed sustain. Later patches reworked the economy again.

流放之路 2 0.1.0c 战利品与通货补丁:圣言/宝石匠掉率、混沌/工匠重分配与界石前缀

zh-cnpublishedverified0.1.0cbalance

0.1.0c 经济补丁提高了圣言石与宝石匠 prism 的掉率,重新分配了混沌石与工匠石,并增强界石前缀以改善早期地图续航。本页记录 0.1.0c 究竟改了什么,以及后续补丁如何再次改动经济。

快速概览

  • 0.1.0c 经济补丁在首发约一周后,着手处理早期经济。它提高了圣言石与宝石匠 prism 的掉率,将混沌石与工匠石重新分配到更合理的位置,并增强界石前缀,让地图续航更可靠。
  • 本页是 0.1.0c 的完整历史:它在经济中改了什么、为何对早期进程重要,以及哪些经济规则仍与当前客户端相似、哪些被后续补丁重建。
  • 0.1.0c 于 2024-12-13 上线,距首发一周
  • 更高的圣言石与宝石匠 prism 掉率
  • 混沌与工匠重分配,外加界石前缀增强

历史提醒

流放之路 2 抢先体验 — 0.1.0c 经济时代

本页当前状态描述均以客户端 0.5.4e 为基准核对。

  • 0.1.0c 是历史经济补丁,而非当前经济指南。它触及的通货掉率与界石规则在抢先体验的第一年里被反复重做。
  • 今天阅读首日的经济指南会误导人:它所描述的圣言、宝石匠、混沌、工匠位置,在 0.1.0c 被改动,之后又被 0.2 至 0.5 再次修改。请把 0.1.0c 视频视为首发经济的记录,而非当前教程。
  • 0.1.0c = 经济起点,而非最终设计
  • 首日经济指南是历史记录,不是当前指南
  • 下列所有当前规则描述均以 0.5.4e 核对

0.1.0c 究竟改了什么

  • 最显眼的经济改动是通货供给:圣言石与宝石匠 prism 都是升级与品质装备的门槛,它们掉落更频繁,玩家不再被硬性卡住。混沌石与工匠石被重新分配到更合适的内容掉落。
  • 在终局侧,界石前缀被增强。更强的后缀意味着地图更可能链向更多地图,改善了 0.1.0 首发所困扰的早期续航。
  • 圣言与宝石匠掉率提升
  • 混沌与工匠重新分配
  • 界石前缀增强以改善续航

版本族时间线

0.1.02024-12-06抢先体验首发首发经济,通货与界石续航粗糙。
0.1.0b2024-12-09热修技能与崩溃修复,尚无经济改动。
0.1.0c2024-12-13经济补丁圣言/宝石匠掉率提升,混沌/工匠重分配,界石前缀增强。
0.2.0+2025-04-04后续主线当前主线(基线 0.5.4e),再次重做经济。

首发经济 vs 0.1.0c 之后

首发当日 0.1.0 经济(0.1.0c 之前)

圣言与宝石匠掉落稀少,混沌与工匠位置尴尬,界石前缀很少能维持地图续航。

0.1.0c 之后

圣言与宝石匠掉落更频繁,混沌与工匠移到更合适的内容,界石前缀增强了续航。后续补丁再次重做经济。

Path of Exile 2 0.1.0d Mechanics Patch: Trigger Gem Energy Rework, Leech Buff & Block/Dodge Changes

enpublishedverified0.1.0dbalance

The 0.1.0d mechanics patch reworked Trigger Gem Energy (Monster Power and Ailment Strength), buffed Leech, changed Block and Dodge, and clarified Trial Keys. This page records exactly what 0.1.0d changed and how later patches moved the mechanics again.

Quick summary

  • The 0.1.0d mechanics patch, less than two weeks after launch, reworked how Trigger Gems gained and spent Energy, rebuilt on the new Monster Power and Ailment Strength stats. It also buffed Leech, adjusted Block and Dodge, and made Trial Keys clearer.
  • This page is the total-entry history of 0.1.0d: what it changed in the mechanical layer, why it mattered for defence and trigger builds, and which mechanics still resemble the current client versus what later patches rebuilt.
  • 0.1.0d landed 2024-12-17, under two weeks after launch
  • Trigger Gem Energy reworked on Monster Power and Ailment Strength
  • Leech buff, Block/Dodge changes, Trial Key clarity

Historical warning

Path of Exile 2 Early Access — the 0.1.0d mechanics era

Current-status claims on this page are measured against client 0.5.4e.

  • 0.1.0d is a historical mechanics patch, not a current mechanics guide. The Trigger Gem Energy, Leech, Block and Dodge rules it touched were reworked repeatedly across the first year of Early Access.
  • Reading a Day-1 trigger or defence guide today is misleading: the Energy model and defence maths it shows were changed by 0.1.0d and then again by 0.2 to 0.5. Treat 0.1.0d footage as a record of the launch mechanics, not as a current tutorial.
  • 0.1.0d = mechanics starting point, not final design
  • Day-1 trigger/defence guides are historical records, not current guides
  • All current-rule claims below are checked against 0.5.4e

What 0.1.0d actually changed

  • The headline mechanical change was Trigger Gem Energy. Instead of the old model, triggers now scaled with Monster Power and Ailment Strength, so the same support behaved differently depending on the content's stats. This mattered most for builds leaning on cast-on-crit or cast-on-ailment setups.
  • Defence and recovery also moved: Leech was buffed so sustain felt better, while Block and Dodge were adjusted to change how often hits were avoided. Finally, Trial Keys were clarified so players understood how to reach the trials.
  • Trigger Gem Energy on Monster Power and Ailment Strength
  • Leech buffed for better sustain
  • Block and Dodge adjusted; Trial Keys clarified

Patch family timeline

0.1.02024-12-06Early Access LaunchLaunch mechanics with the first Trigger Gem and defence model.
0.1.0b2024-12-09HotfixSkill and crash fixes.
0.1.0c2024-12-13Economy PatchLoot and currency rebalance.
0.1.0d2024-12-17Mechanics PatchTrigger Gem Energy rework, Leech buff, Block/Dodge changes, Trial Key clarity.

Launch mechanics vs after 0.1.0d

Launch-day 0.1.0 mechanics (before 0.1.0d)

Trigger Gems used the old Energy model, Leech was weaker, and Block/Dodge and trial access were less clear.

After 0.1.0d

Triggers scaled with Monster Power and Ailment Strength, Leech was buffed, Block/Dodge adjusted, and Trial Keys clarified. Later patches refined these again.

Current applicability (0.5.4e)
